  55 |     |             fees[i] = fee;
  56 |     |             vaultCredits[i] = need;
  57 |     |         }
  58 |     |         if (hasNative) {
  59 |     |             _pullNativeMint(nativeTotal);
  60 |     |         } else {
  61 |     |             LibCurrency.assertZeroMsgValue();
  62 |     |         }
  63 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
  64 |     |             address asset = idx.assets[i];
  65 |     |             uint256 need = required[i];
  66 |     |             s().vaultBalances[indexId][asset] = vaultBalancesBefore[i] + need;
  67 |     |             _distributeIndexFee(indexId, idx, asset, fees[i], feeIndexShareBps);
  68 |     |         }
  69 |     | 
  70 |     |         uint256 totalSupplyBefore = idx.totalUnits;
  71 |     |         if (totalSupplyBefore == 0) {
  72 |     |             minted = units;
  73 |     |         } else {
  74 |     |             minted = type(uint256).max;
  75 |     |             for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
  76 |     |                 uint256 balanceBefore = vaultBalancesBefore[i];
  77 |     |                 require(balanceBefore > 0, "EqualIndex: zero NAV asset");
  78 |     |                 uint256 mintedForAsset = (vaultCredits[i] * totalSupplyBefore) / balanceBefore;
  79 |     |                 if (mintedForAsset < minted) minted = mintedForAsset;
  80 |     |             }
  81 |     |             if (minted == 0) revert InvalidUnits();
  82 |     |         }
  83 |     | 
  84 |     |         idx.totalUnits = totalSupplyBefore + minted;
  85 |     |         IndexToken(idx.token).mintIndexUnits(to, minted);
  86 |     |         IndexToken(idx.token).recordMintDetails(to, minted, idx.assets, required, fees, 0);
  87 |     | 
  88 |     |         emit LibEqualIndex.Minted(indexId, to, minted, required);
  89 |     |     }

  91 |     |     /// @notice Burn index tokens and redeem bundle share + fee pot share, minus burn fee.
  92 |     |     function burn(uint256 indexId, uint256 units, address to)
  93 |     |         external

  95 |     |         nonReentrant
  96 |     |         indexExists(indexId)
  97 |     |         returns (uint256[] memory assetsOut)
  98 |     |     {

 112 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {

 143 |     |     /// @notice Flash borrow proportional bundle amounts for a given unit amount.
 144 |     |     function flashLoan(uint256 indexId, uint256 units, address receiver, bytes calldata data)
 145 |     |         external
 146 |     |         payable
 147 |     |         nonReentrant
 148 |     |         indexExists(indexId)
 149 |     |     {
 150 | *   |         LibCurrency.assertZeroMsgValue();
 151 |     |         if (units == 0 || units % LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE != 0) revert InvalidUnits();
 152 |     |         Index storage idx = s().indexes[indexId];
 153 |     |         _requireIndexActive(idx, indexId);
 154 |     |         uint256 totalSupply = idx.totalUnits;
 155 |     |         if (totalSupply == 0 || units > totalSupply) revert InvalidUnits();
 156 |     | 
 157 |     |         uint256 len = idx.assets.length;
 158 |     |         address[] memory assets = idx.assets;
 159 |     |         uint256[] memory loanAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 160 |     |         uint256[] memory fees = new uint256[](len);
 161 |     |         uint256[] memory contractBalancesBefore = new uint256[](len);
 162 |     | 
 163 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 164 |     |             address asset = assets[i];
 165 |     |             contractBalancesBefore[i] = LibCurrency.balanceOfSelf(asset);
 166 |     |             uint256 vaultBalance = s().vaultBalances[indexId][asset];
 167 |     |             uint256 navShare = Math.mulDiv(vaultBalance, units, totalSupply);
 168 |     |             loanAmounts[i] = navShare;
 169 |     |             uint256 fee = Math.mulDiv(navShare, idx.flashFeeBps, 10_000);
 170 |     |             fees[i] = fee;
 171 |     |             s().vaultBalances[indexId][asset] = vaultBalance - navShare;
 172 |     |             if (navShare > 0) {
 173 |     |                 LibCurrency.transfer(asset, receiver, navShare);
 174 |     |             }
 175 |     |         }
 176 |     | 
 177 |     |         IEqualIndexFlashReceiver(receiver).onEqualIndexFlashLoan(indexId, units, assets, loanAmounts, fees, data);
 178 |     | 
 179 |     |         _finalizeFlashLoan(indexId, idx, assets, loanAmounts, fees, contractBalancesBefore);
 180 |     | 
 181 |     |         emit LibEqualIndex.FlashLoaned(indexId, receiver, units, loanAmounts, fees);
 182 |     |     }
 183 |     | 
 184 |     |     function _finalizeFlashLoan(
 185 |     |         uint256 indexId,
 186 |     |         Index storage idx,
 187 |     |         address[] memory assets,
 188 |     |         uint256[] memory loanAmounts,
 189 |     |         uint256[] memory fees,
 190 |     |         uint256[] memory contractBalancesBefore
 191 |     |     ) internal {
 192 |     |         uint16 poolFeeShareBps = _poolFeeShareBps();
 193 |     |         uint256 len = assets.length;
 194 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 195 |     |             _settleFlashLoanFee(
 196 |     |                 indexId,
 197 |     |                 idx,
 198 |     |                 assets[i],
 199 |     |                 loanAmounts[i],
 200 |     |                 fees[i],
 201 |     |                 contractBalancesBefore[i],
 202 |     |                 poolFeeShareBps
 203 |     |             );
 204 |     |         }
 205 |     |     }
 206 |     | 
 207 |     |     function _settleFlashLoanFee(
 208 |     |         uint256 indexId,
 209 |     |         Index storage idx,
 210 |     |         address asset,
 211 |     |         uint256 loanAmount,
 212 |     |         uint256 fee,
 213 |     |         uint256 balanceBefore,
 214 |     |         uint16 poolFeeShareBps
 215 |     |     ) internal {
 216 |     |         uint256 expectedBalance = balanceBefore + fee;
 217 |     |         uint256 actualBalance = LibCurrency.balanceOfSelf(asset);
 218 |     |         if (actualBalance < expectedBalance) {
 219 |     |             revert FlashLoanUnderpaid(indexId, asset, expectedBalance, actualBalance);
 220 |     |         }
 221 |     |         s().vaultBalances[indexId][asset] += loanAmount;
 222 |     |         if (LibCurrency.isNative(asset) && fee > 0) {
 223 |     |             LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal += fee;
 224 |     |         }
 225 |     |         _distributeIndexFee(indexId, idx, asset, fee, poolFeeShareBps);
 226 |     |     }
 227 |     | 
 228 |     |     function _pullNativeMint(uint256 amount) internal {
 229 |     |         if (amount == 0) {
 230 |     |             LibCurrency.assertZeroMsgValue();
 231 |     |             return;
 232 |     |         }
 233 |     |         if (msg.value == 0) {
 234 |     |             uint256 availableNative = LibCurrency.nativeAvailable();
 235 |     |             if (amount > availableNative) {
 236 |     |                 revert InsufficientPoolLiquidity(amount, availableNative);
 237 |     |             }
 238 |     |             LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal += amount;
 239 |     |             return;
 240 |     |         }
 241 |     |         if (msg.value != amount) {
 242 |     |             revert UnexpectedMsgValue(msg.value);
 243 |     |         }
 244 |     |         uint256 availableAfterValue = LibCurrency.nativeAvailable();
 245 |     |         uint256 preAvailable = availableAfterValue > msg.value ? availableAfterValue - msg.value : 0;
 246 |     |         LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal += amount;
 247 |     |         if (preAvailable >= amount) {
 248 |     |             (bool success,) = msg.sender.call{value: msg.value}("");
 249 |     |             if (!success) {
 250 |     |                 // Refunds are best-effort; keep funds if receiver rejects.
 251 |     |             }
 252 |     |         }
 253 |     |     }
 254 |     | 
 255 |     |     /// @dev Distributes index fee between Fee Pot and pool fee routing share.
 256 |     |     /// Uses feeIndexShareBps for the pool share, remainder goes to the Fee Pot.
 257 |     |     function _distributeIndexFee(
 258 |     |         uint256 indexId,
 259 |     |         Index storage idx,
 260 |     |         address asset,
 261 |     |         uint256 fee,
 262 |     |         uint16 feeIndexShareBps
 263 |     |     ) internal {
 264 |     |         if (fee == 0) return;
 265 |     | 
 266 |     |         // 1. Pool share routed through standard fee router (FI/ACI/Treasury).
 267 |     |         uint256 poolShare = Math.mulDiv(fee, feeIndexShareBps, 10_000);
 268 |     |         // 2. Fee pot share (index holders)
 269 |     |         uint256 potFee = fee - poolShare;
 270 |     |         if (potFee > 0) {
 271 |     |             s().feePots[indexId][asset] += potFee;
 272 |     |         }
 273 |     | 
 274 |     |         if (poolShare > 0) {
 275 |     |             uint256 poolId = LibAppStorage.s().assetToPoolId[asset];
 276 |     |             if (poolId == 0) revert NoPoolForAsset(asset);
 277 |     |             Types.PoolData storage pool = LibAppStorage.s().pools[poolId];
 278 |     |             pool.trackedBalance += poolShare;
 279 |     |             // Count the incoming fee as backing even if tracked balance is slightly short.
 280 |     |             if (LibCurrency.isNative(asset)) {
 281 |     |                 _routeIndexPoolShareNative(pool, poolId, poolShare);
 282 |     |             } else {
 283 |     |                 LibFeeRouter.routeSamePool(poolId, poolShare, INDEX_FEE_SOURCE, true, poolShare);
 284 |     |             }
 285 |     |         }
 286 |     |     }
 287 |     | 
 288 |     |     function _routeIndexPoolShareNative(Types.PoolData storage pool, uint256 pid, uint256 amount) internal {
 289 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 290 |     |         (uint256 toTreasury, uint256 toActiveCredit, uint256 toFeeIndex) = LibFeeRouter.previewSplit(amount);
 291 |     |         if (toTreasury > 0) {
 292 |     |             address treasury = LibAppStorage.treasuryAddress(LibAppStorage.s());
 293 |     |             if (treasury != address(0)) {
 294 |     |                 uint256 tracked = pool.trackedBalance;
 295 |     |                 if (tracked < toTreasury) {
 296 |     |                     revert InsufficientPrincipal(toTreasury, tracked);
 297 |     |                 }
 298 |     |                 uint256 contractBal = LibCurrency.balanceOfSelf(pool.underlying);
 299 |     |                 if (contractBal < toTreasury) {
 300 |     |                     revert InsufficientPrincipal(toTreasury, contractBal);
 301 |     |                 }
 302 |     |                 pool.trackedBalance = tracked - toTreasury;
 303 |     |                 LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal -= toTreasury;
 304 |     |                 LibCurrency.transfer(pool.underlying, treasury, toTreasury);
 305 |     |             }
 306 |     |         }
 307 |     |         if (toActiveCredit > 0) {
 308 |     |             LibFeeRouter.accrueActiveCredit(pid, toActiveCredit, INDEX_FEE_SOURCE, amount);
 309 |     |         }
 310 |     |         if (toFeeIndex > 0) {
 311 |     |             LibFeeIndex.accrueWithSourceUsingBacking(pid, toFeeIndex, INDEX_FEE_SOURCE, amount);
 312 |     |         }
 313 |     |     }
 314 |     | }

 105 |     |     function _getIndexAssetsPaginated(
 106 |     |         Index storage idx,
 107 |     |         uint256 offset,
 108 |     |         uint256 limit
 109 |     |     )
 110 |     |         internal
 111 |     |         view
 112 |     |         returns (
 113 |     |             address[] memory assets,
 114 |     |             uint256[] memory bundleAmounts,
 115 |     |             uint16[] memory mintFeeBps,
 116 |     |             uint16[] memory burnFeeBps
 117 |     |         )
 118 |     |     {
 119 |     |         uint256 total = idx.assets.length;
 120 |     |         if (offset >= total) {
 121 |     |             return (new address[](0), new uint256[](0), new uint16[](0), new uint16[](0));
 122 |     |         }
 123 |     | 
 124 |     |         uint256 remaining = total - offset;
 125 |     |         if (limit == 0 || limit > remaining) {
 126 |     |             limit = remaining;
 127 |     |         }
 128 |     | 
 129 |     |         assets = new address[](limit);
 130 |     |         bundleAmounts = new uint256[](limit);
 131 |     |         mintFeeBps = new uint16[](limit);
 132 |     |         burnFeeBps = new uint16[](limit);
 133 |     | 
 134 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
 135 |     |             uint256 idxOffset = offset + i;
 136 |     |             assets[i] = idx.assets[idxOffset];
 137 |     |             bundleAmounts[i] = idx.bundleAmounts[idxOffset];
 138 |     |             mintFeeBps[i] = idx.mintFeeBps[idxOffset];
 139 |     |             burnFeeBps[i] = idx.burnFeeBps[idxOffset];
 140 |     |         }
 141 |     |     }

  64 |     |     ) ERC20(name_, symbol_) ERC20Permit(name_) {
  65 |     |         if (minter_ == address(0)) revert InvalidMinter();
  66 |     |         require(assets_.length == bundleAmounts_.length, "IndexToken: length mismatch");
  67 |     |         require(assets_.length > 0, "IndexToken: empty bundle");
  68 |     | 
  69 |     |         minter = minter_;
  70 |     |         indexId = indexId_;
  71 |     |         _assets = assets_;
  72 |     |         _bundleAmounts = bundleAmounts_;
  73 |     |         bundleCount = assets_.length;
  74 |     |         flashFeeBps = flashFeeBps_;
  75 |     |         bundleHash = keccak256(abi.encode(assets_, bundleAmounts_));
  76 |     |     }
  77 |     | 
  78 |     |     // --- Mint/burn control ---
  79 |     | 
  80 |     |     function mintIndexUnits(address to, uint256 amount) external nonReentrant onlyMinter {
  81 |     |         _mint(to, amount);
  82 |     |     }
  83 |     | 
  84 |     |     function burnIndexUnits(address from, uint256 amount) external nonReentrant onlyMinter {
  85 |     |         _burn(from, amount);
  86 |     |     }
  87 |     | 
  88 |     |     function recordMintDetails(
  89 |     |         address user,
  90 |     |         uint256 units,
  91 |     |         address[] calldata assets_,
  92 |     |         uint256[] calldata assetAmounts,
  93 |     |         uint256[] calldata feeAmounts,
  94 |     |         uint256 feeUnits
  95 |     |     ) external onlyMinter {
  96 |     |         if (feeUnits == 0 && feeAmounts.length == _bundleAmounts.length) {
  97 |     |             feeUnits = _feeAmountsToUnits(feeAmounts);
  98 |     |         }
  99 |     |         totalMintFeesCollected += feeUnits;
 100 |     |         emit MintDetails(user, units, assets_, assetAmounts, feeAmounts);
 101 |     |     }
 102 |     | 
 103 |     |     function recordBurnDetails(
 104 |     |         address user,
 105 |     |         uint256 units,
 106 |     |         address[] calldata assets_,
 107 |     |         uint256[] calldata assetAmounts,
 108 |     |         uint256[] calldata feeAmounts,
 109 |     |         uint256 feeUnits
 110 |     |     ) external onlyMinter {
 111 |     |         if (feeUnits == 0 && feeAmounts.length == _bundleAmounts.length) {
 112 |     |             feeUnits = _feeAmountsToUnits(feeAmounts);
 113 |     |         }
 114 |     |         totalBurnFeesCollected += feeUnits;
 115 |     |         emit BurnDetails(user, units, assets_, assetAmounts, feeAmounts);
 116 |     |     }

 119 |     |         flashFeeBps = newFlashFeeBps;
 120 |     |     }

 122 |     |     // --- Introspection helpers ---

 124 |     |     /// @dev Internal helper for paginating bundle configuration
 125 |     |     function _sliceBundle(uint256 offset, uint256 limit)
 126 |     |         internal
 127 |     |         view
 128 |     |         returns (address[] memory assetsSlice, uint256[] memory bundleSlice)
 129 |     |     {
 130 |     |         uint256 total = bundleCount;
 131 |     |         if (offset >= total) {
 132 |     |             return (new address[](0), new uint256[](0));
 133 |     |         }
 134 |     | 
 135 |     |         uint256 remaining = total - offset;
 136 |     |         if (limit == 0 || limit > remaining) {
 137 |     |             limit = remaining;
 138 |     |         }
 139 |     | 
 140 |     |         assetsSlice = new address[](limit);
 141 |     |         bundleSlice = new uint256[](limit);
 142 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
 143 |     |             uint256 idx = offset + i;
 144 |     |             assetsSlice[i] = _assets[idx];
 145 |     |             bundleSlice[i] = _bundleAmounts[idx];
 146 |     |         }
 147 |     |     }
 148 |     | 
 149 |     |     function assets() external view returns (address[] memory) {
 150 |     |         return _assets;
 151 |     |     }
 152 |     | 
 153 |     |     function bundleAmounts() external view returns (uint256[] memory) {
 154 |     |         return _bundleAmounts;
 155 |     |     }
 156 |     | 
 157 |     |     /// @notice Get paginated list of index assets
 158 |     |     /// @param offset Starting asset index (0-based)
 159 |     |     /// @param limit Maximum number of assets to return (0 = until end)
 160 |     |     function assetsPaginated(uint256 offset, uint256 limit) external view returns (address[] memory assetsOut) {
 161 |     |         (assetsOut,) = _sliceBundle(offset, limit);
 162 |     |     }
 163 |     | 
 164 |     |     /// @notice Get paginated list of bundle amounts
 165 |     |     /// @param offset Starting bundle index (0-based)
 166 |     |     /// @param limit Maximum number of bundle entries to return (0 = until end)
 167 |     |     function bundleAmountsPaginated(uint256 offset, uint256 limit)
 168 |     |         external
 169 |     |         view
 170 |     |         returns (uint256[] memory bundleOut)
 171 |     |     {
 172 |     |         (, bundleOut) = _sliceBundle(offset, limit);
 173 |     |     }

 175 |     |     function previewMint(uint256 units)
 176 |     |         external
 177 |     |         view
 178 |     |         returns (address[] memory assetsOut, uint256[] memory required, uint256[] memory feeAmounts)
 179 |     |     {
 180 |     |         assetsOut = _assets;
 181 |     |         uint256 len = _bundleAmounts.length;
 182 |     |         required = new uint256[](len);
 183 |     |         feeAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 184 |     |         IEqualIndexData.Index memory idx = IEqualIndexData(minter).getIndex(indexId);
 185 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 186 |     |             uint256 need = Math.mulDiv(_bundleAmounts[i], units, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE);
 187 |     |             uint256 fee = Math.mulDiv(need, idx.mintFeeBps[i], 10_000);
 188 |     |             required[i] = need + fee;
 189 |     |             feeAmounts[i] = fee;
 190 |     |         }
 191 |     |     }
 192 |     | 
 193 |     |     /// @notice Paginated preview of mint requirements for a given unit amount
 194 |     |     /// @param units Number of index units to mint
 195 |     |     /// @param offset Starting asset index (0-based)
 196 |     |     /// @param limit Maximum number of assets to return (0 = until end)
 197 |     |     function previewMintPaginated(uint256 units, uint256 offset, uint256 limit)
 198 |     |         external
 199 |     |         view
 200 |     |         returns (address[] memory assetsOut, uint256[] memory required, uint256[] memory feeAmounts)
 201 |     |     {
 202 |     |         uint256[] memory bundleSlice;
 203 |     |         (assetsOut, bundleSlice) = _sliceBundle(offset, limit);
 204 |     |         uint256 len = bundleSlice.length;
 205 |     |         required = new uint256[](len);
 206 |     |         feeAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 207 |     |         IEqualIndexData.Index memory idx = IEqualIndexData(minter).getIndex(indexId);
 208 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 209 |     |             uint256 need = Math.mulDiv(bundleSlice[i], units, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE);
 210 |     |             uint256 fee = Math.mulDiv(need, idx.mintFeeBps[offset + i], 10_000);
 211 |     |             required[i] = need + fee;
 212 |     |             feeAmounts[i] = fee;
 213 |     |         }
 214 |     |     }
 215 |     | 
 216 |     |     /// @notice Preview redemption at current NAV (includes accumulated fees in vault)
 217 |     |     function previewRedeem(uint256 units)
 218 |     |         external
 219 |     |         view
 220 |     |         returns (address[] memory assetsOut, uint256[] memory netOut, uint256[] memory feeAmounts)
 221 |     |     {
 222 |     |         assetsOut = _assets;
 223 |     |         uint256 len = _bundleAmounts.length;
 224 |     |         netOut = new uint256[](len);
 225 |     |         feeAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 226 |     |         
 227 |     |         IEqualIndexData.Index memory idx = IEqualIndexData(minter).getIndex(indexId);
 228 |     |         uint256 totalSupply = totalSupply();
 229 |     |         
 230 |     |         if (totalSupply == 0) {
 231 |     |             // No supply, return base bundle
 232 |     |             for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 233 |     |                 uint256 gross = Math.mulDiv(_bundleAmounts[i], units, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE);
 234 |     |                 uint256 burnFee = Math.mulDiv(gross, idx.burnFeeBps[i], 10_000);
 235 |     |                 netOut[i] = gross - burnFee;
 236 |     |                 feeAmounts[i] = burnFee;
 237 |     |             }
 238 |     |         } else {
 239 |     |             // Return proportional share of vault (current NAV)
 240 |     |             for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 241 |     |                 uint256 vaultBalance = IEqualIndexData(minter).getVaultBalance(indexId, _assets[i]);
 242 |     |                 uint256 potBalance = IEqualIndexData(minter).getFeePot(indexId, _assets[i]);
 243 |     |                 uint256 navShare = Math.mulDiv(vaultBalance, units, totalSupply);
 244 |     |                 uint256 potShare = Math.mulDiv(potBalance, units, totalSupply);
 245 |     |                 uint256 gross = navShare + potShare;
 246 |     |                 uint256 burnFee = Math.mulDiv(gross, idx.burnFeeBps[i], 10_000);
 247 |     |                 netOut[i] = gross - burnFee;
 248 |     |                 feeAmounts[i] = burnFee;
 249 |     |             }
 250 |     |         }
 251 |     |     }
 252 |     | 
 253 |     |     /// @notice Paginated preview of redemption at current NAV
 254 |     |     /// @param units Number of index units to redeem
 255 |     |     /// @param offset Starting asset index (0-based)
 256 |     |     /// @param limit Maximum number of assets to return (0 = until end)
 257 |     |     function previewRedeemPaginated(uint256 units, uint256 offset, uint256 limit)
 258 |     |         external
 259 |     |         view
 260 |     |         returns (address[] memory assetsOut, uint256[] memory netOut, uint256[] memory feeAmounts)
 261 |     |     {
 262 |     |         uint256[] memory bundleSlice;
 263 |     |         (assetsOut, bundleSlice) = _sliceBundle(offset, limit);
 264 |     |         uint256 len = bundleSlice.length;
 265 |     |         netOut = new uint256[](len);
 266 |     |         feeAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 267 |     | 
 268 |     |         // Maintain parity with previewRedeem by consulting index metadata
 269 |     |         IEqualIndexData.Index memory idx = IEqualIndexData(minter).getIndex(indexId);
 270 |     |         uint256 totalSupply = totalSupply();
 271 |     | 
 272 |     |         if (totalSupply == 0) {
 273 |     |             // No supply, return base bundle for this slice
 274 |     |             for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 275 |     |                 uint256 gross = Math.mulDiv(bundleSlice[i], units, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE);
 276 |     |                 uint256 burnFee = Math.mulDiv(gross, idx.burnFeeBps[offset + i], 10_000);
 277 |     |                 netOut[i] = gross - burnFee;
 278 |     |                 feeAmounts[i] = burnFee;
 279 |     |             }
 280 |     |         } else {
 281 |     |             // Return proportional share of vault (current NAV) for this slice
 282 |     |             for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 283 |     |                 uint256 vaultBalance = IEqualIndexData(minter).getVaultBalance(indexId, assetsOut[i]);
 284 |     |                 uint256 potBalance = IEqualIndexData(minter).getFeePot(indexId, assetsOut[i]);
 285 |     |                 uint256 navShare = Math.mulDiv(vaultBalance, units, totalSupply);
 286 |     |                 uint256 potShare = Math.mulDiv(potBalance, units, totalSupply);
 287 |     |                 uint256 gross = navShare + potShare;
 288 |     |                 uint256 burnFee = Math.mulDiv(gross, idx.burnFeeBps[offset + i], 10_000);
 289 |     |                 netOut[i] = gross - burnFee;
 290 |     |                 feeAmounts[i] = burnFee;
 291 |     |             }
 292 |     |         }
 293 |     |     }
 294 |     | 
 295 |     |     function previewFlashLoan(uint256 units)
 296 |     |         external
 297 |     |         view
 298 |     |         returns (address[] memory assetsOut, uint256[] memory loanAmounts, uint256[] memory feeAmounts)
 299 |     |     {
 300 |     |         assetsOut = _assets;
 301 |     |         uint256 len = _bundleAmounts.length;
 302 |     |         loanAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 303 |     |         feeAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 304 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 305 |     |             uint256 amount = Math.mulDiv(_bundleAmounts[i], units, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE);
 306 |     |             loanAmounts[i] = amount;
 307 |     |             feeAmounts[i] = (amount * flashFeeBps) / 10_000;
 308 |     |         }
 309 |     |     }
 310 |     | 
 311 |     |     /// @notice Paginated preview of flash loan bundle amounts and fees
 312 |     |     /// @param units Number of index units to borrow
 313 |     |     /// @param offset Starting asset index (0-based)
 314 |     |     /// @param limit Maximum number of assets to return (0 = until end)
 315 |     |     function previewFlashLoanPaginated(uint256 units, uint256 offset, uint256 limit)
 316 |     |         external
 317 |     |         view
 318 |     |         returns (address[] memory assetsOut, uint256[] memory loanAmounts, uint256[] memory feeAmounts)
 319 |     |     {
 320 |     |         uint256[] memory bundleSlice;
 321 |     |         (assetsOut, bundleSlice) = _sliceBundle(offset, limit);
 322 |     |         uint256 len = bundleSlice.length;
 323 |     |         loanAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 324 |     |         feeAmounts = new uint256[](len);
 325 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 326 |     |             uint256 amount = Math.mulDiv(bundleSlice[i], units, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE);
 327 |     |             loanAmounts[i] = amount;
 328 |     |             feeAmounts[i] = (amount * flashFeeBps) / 10_000;
 329 |     |         }
 330 |     |     }

 332 |     |     function snapshot()
 333 |     |         external
 334 |     |         view
 335 |     |         returns (
 336 |     |             address[] memory __assets,
 337 |     |             uint256[] memory __bundleAmounts,
 338 |     |             uint256 totalUnits,
 339 |     |             uint256 _flashFeeBps
 340 |     |         )
 341 |     |     {
 342 |     |         __assets = _assets;
 343 |     |         __bundleAmounts = _bundleAmounts;
 344 |     |         totalUnits = totalSupply();
 345 |     |         _flashFeeBps = flashFeeBps;
 346 |     |     }

 348 |     |     /// @notice Returns true if vault balances cover required bundles for total supply.
 349 |     |     function isSolvent() external view returns (bool) {
 350 |     |         uint256 supply = totalSupply();
 351 |     |         address[] memory assetList = _assets;
 352 |     |         uint256 len = assetList.length;
 353 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 354 |     |             uint256 required = Math.mulDiv(_bundleAmounts[i], supply, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE);
 355 |     |             uint256 bal = IEqualIndexData(minter).getVaultBalance(indexId, assetList[i]);
 356 |     |             if (bal < required) return false;
 357 |     |         }
 358 |     |         return true;
 359 |     |     }
 360 |     | 
 361 |     |     /// @dev Convert per-asset fee amounts into the equivalent index units using bundle ratios.
 362 |     |     function _feeAmountsToUnits(uint256[] calldata feeAmounts) internal view returns (uint256 feeUnits) {
 363 |     |         uint256 len = _bundleAmounts.length;
 364 |     |         if (feeAmounts.length != len) return 0;
 365 |     |         bool hasFee;
 366 |     |         feeUnits = type(uint256).max;
 367 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 368 |     |             uint256 fee = feeAmounts[i];
 369 |     |             if (fee == 0) continue;
 370 |     |             hasFee = true;
 371 |     |             uint256 unitsForAsset = Math.mulDiv(fee, LibEqualIndex.INDEX_SCALE, _bundleAmounts[i]);
 372 |     |             if (unitsForAsset < feeUnits) {
 373 |     |                 feeUnits = unitsForAsset;
 374 |     |             }
 375 |     |         }
 376 |     |         if (!hasFee) {
 377 |     |             return 0;
 378 |     |         }
 379 |     |     }
 380 |     | }

/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibActiveCreditIndex.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import {Math} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol";
   5 |     | import {LibAppStorage} from "./LibAppStorage.sol";
   6 |     | import {LibMaintenance} from "./LibMaintenance.sol";
   7 |     | import {Types} from "./Types.sol";
   8 |     | 
   9 |     | /// @notice Active Credit index accounting with 24h time-gated eligibility
  10 |     | library LibActiveCreditIndex {
  11 |     |     uint256 public constant INDEX_SCALE = 1e18;
  12 |     |     uint256 public constant TIME_GATE = 24 hours;
  13 |     |     uint256 internal constant BUCKET_SIZE = 1 hours;
  14 |     |     uint8 internal constant BUCKET_COUNT = 24;
  15 |     | 
  16 |     |     event ActiveCreditIndexAccrued(uint256 indexed pid, uint256 amount, uint256 delta, uint256 newIndex, bytes32 source);
  17 |     |     event ActiveCreditSettled(
  18 |     |         uint256 indexed pid,
  19 |     |         bytes32 indexed user,
  20 |     |         uint256 prevIndex,
  21 |     |         uint256 newIndex,
  22 |     |         uint256 addedYield,
  23 |     |         uint256 totalAccruedYield
  24 |     |     );
  25 |     | 
  26 |     |     event ActiveCreditTimingUpdated(
  27 |     |         uint256 indexed pid,
  28 |     |         bytes32 indexed user,
  29 |     |         bool isDebtState,
  30 |     |         uint40 startTime,
  31 |     |         uint256 principal,
  32 |     |         bool isMature
  33 |     |     );
  34 |     | 
  35 |     |     /// @notice Compute time credit toward maturity (capped at TIME_GATE).
  36 |     |     function timeCredit(Types.ActiveCreditState storage state) internal view returns (uint256) {
  37 |     |         if (state.principal == 0 || block.timestamp <= state.startTime) return 0;
  38 |     |         uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - state.startTime;
  39 |     |         return elapsed > TIME_GATE ? TIME_GATE : elapsed;
  40 |     |     }
  41 |     | 
  42 |     |     /// @notice Calculate active weight for a state (0 until mature).
  43 |     |     function activeWeight(Types.ActiveCreditState storage state) internal view returns (uint256) {
  44 |     |         return timeCredit(state) < TIME_GATE ? 0 : state.principal;
  45 |     |     }
  46 |     | 
  47 |     |     /// @notice Apply weighted dilution on principal increase and update startTime.
  48 |     |     function applyWeightedIncrease(Types.ActiveCreditState storage state, uint256 addedPrincipal) internal {
  49 |     |         if (addedPrincipal == 0) return;
  50 |     |         uint256 oldPrincipal = state.principal;
  51 |     |         if (oldPrincipal == 0) {
  52 |     |             state.principal = addedPrincipal;
  53 |     |             state.startTime = uint40(block.timestamp);
  54 |     |             return;
  55 |     |         }
  56 |     |         uint256 oldCredit = timeCredit(state);
  57 |     |         uint256 total = oldPrincipal + addedPrincipal;
  58 |     |         uint256 newCredit = Math.mulDiv(oldPrincipal, oldCredit, total);
  59 |     |         if (newCredit > TIME_GATE) newCredit = TIME_GATE;
  60 |     |         state.principal = total;
  61 |     |         state.startTime = uint40(block.timestamp - newCredit);
  62 |     |     }
  63 |     | 
  64 |     |     /// @notice Apply weighted dilution and emit timing gate status for the updated state.
  65 |     |     function applyWeightedIncreaseWithGate(
  66 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
  67 |     |         Types.ActiveCreditState storage state,
  68 |     |         uint256 addedPrincipal,
  69 |     |         uint256 pid,
  70 |     |         bytes32 user,
  71 |     |         bool isDebtState
  72 |     |     ) internal {
  73 |     |         _rollMatured(p);
  74 |     |         _removeFromBase(p, state, state.principal);
  75 |     |         applyWeightedIncrease(state, addedPrincipal);
  76 |     |         _scheduleState(p, state);
  77 |     |         emit ActiveCreditTimingUpdated(pid, user, isDebtState, state.startTime, state.principal, _isMature(state));
  78 |     |     }
  79 |     | 
  80 |     |     /// @notice Reset state when principal is zero.
  81 |     |     function resetIfZero(Types.ActiveCreditState storage state) internal {
  82 |     |         if (state.principal == 0) {
  83 |     |             state.startTime = 0;
  84 |     |             state.indexSnapshot = 0;
  85 |     |         }
  86 |     |     }
  87 |     | 
  88 |     |     /// @notice Reset state when principal is zero and emit timing gate status.
  89 |     |     function resetIfZeroWithGate(
  90 |     |         Types.ActiveCreditState storage state,
  91 |     |         uint256 pid,
  92 |     |         bytes32 user,
  93 |     |         bool isDebtState
  94 |     |     ) internal {
  95 |     |         if (state.principal == 0) {
  96 |     |             state.startTime = 0;
  97 |     |             state.indexSnapshot = 0;
  98 |     |             emit ActiveCreditTimingUpdated(pid, user, isDebtState, 0, 0, false);
  99 |     |             return;
 100 |     |         }
 101 |     |         uint256 principalBefore = state.principal;
 102 |     |         resetIfZero(state);
 103 |     |         if (principalBefore != 0 && state.principal == 0) {
 104 |     |             emit ActiveCreditTimingUpdated(pid, user, isDebtState, state.startTime, state.principal, false);
 105 |     |         }
 106 |     |     }
 107 |     | 
 108 |     |     function applyPrincipalDecrease(
 109 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 110 |     |         Types.ActiveCreditState storage state,
 111 |     |         uint256 decrease
 112 |     |     ) internal {
 113 |     |         if (decrease == 0) return;
 114 |     |         _rollMatured(p);
 115 |     |         _removeFromBase(p, state, decrease);
 116 |     |         if (state.principal <= decrease) {
 117 |     |             state.principal = 0;
 118 |     |         } else {
 119 |     |             state.principal -= decrease;
 120 |     |         }
 121 |     |     }
 122 |     | 
 123 |     |     function applyEncumbranceDelta(
 124 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 125 |     |         uint256 pid,
 126 |     |         bytes32 user,
 127 |     |         uint256 beforeEncumbrance,
 128 |     |         uint256 afterEncumbrance
 129 |     |     ) internal {
 130 |     |         if (beforeEncumbrance == afterEncumbrance) return;
 131 |     |         if (afterEncumbrance > beforeEncumbrance) {
 132 |     |             _increaseEncumbrance(p, pid, user, afterEncumbrance - beforeEncumbrance);
 133 |     |         } else {
 134 |     |             _decreaseEncumbrance(p, pid, user, beforeEncumbrance - afterEncumbrance);
 135 |     |         }
 136 |     |     }
 137 |     | 
 138 |     |     function applyEncumbranceIncrease(
 139 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 140 |     |         uint256 pid,
 141 |     |         bytes32 user,
 142 |     |         uint256 amount
 143 |     |     ) internal {
 144 |     |         _increaseEncumbrance(p, pid, user, amount);
 145 |     |     }
 146 |     | 
 147 |     |     function applyEncumbranceDecrease(
 148 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 149 |     |         uint256 pid,
 150 |     |         bytes32 user,
 151 |     |         uint256 amount
 152 |     |     ) internal {
 153 |     |         _decreaseEncumbrance(p, pid, user, amount);
 154 |     |     }
 155 |     | 
 156 |     |     function _increaseEncumbrance(
 157 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 158 |     |         uint256 pid,
 159 |     |         bytes32 user,
 160 |     |         uint256 amount
 161 |     |     ) private {
 162 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 163 |     |         p.activeCreditPrincipalTotal += amount;
 164 |     |         Types.ActiveCreditState storage enc = p.userActiveCreditStateEncumbrance[user];
 165 |     |         applyWeightedIncreaseWithGate(p, enc, amount, pid, user, false);
 166 |     |         enc.indexSnapshot = p.activeCreditIndex;
 167 |     |     }
 168 |     | 
 169 |     |     function _decreaseEncumbrance(
 170 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 171 |     |         uint256 pid,
 172 |     |         bytes32 user,
 173 |     |         uint256 amount
 174 |     |     ) private {
 175 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 176 |     |         Types.ActiveCreditState storage enc = p.userActiveCreditStateEncumbrance[user];
 177 |     |         uint256 principalBefore = enc.principal;
 178 |     |         uint256 decrease = principalBefore >= amount ? amount : principalBefore;
 179 |     |         if (p.activeCreditPrincipalTotal >= decrease) {
 180 |     |             p.activeCreditPrincipalTotal -= decrease;
 181 |     |         } else {
 182 |     |             p.activeCreditPrincipalTotal = 0;
 183 |     |         }
 184 |     |         applyPrincipalDecrease(p, enc, decrease);
 185 |     |         if (principalBefore <= amount || enc.principal == 0) {
 186 |     |             resetIfZeroWithGate(enc, pid, user, false);
 187 |     |         } else {
 188 |     |             enc.indexSnapshot = p.activeCreditIndex;
 189 |     |         }
 190 |     |     }
 191 |     | 
 192 |     |     function trackState(Types.PoolData storage p, Types.ActiveCreditState storage state) internal {
 193 |     |         if (state.principal == 0) return;
 194 |     |         _rollMatured(p);
 195 |     |         _scheduleState(p, state);
 196 |     |     }
 197 |     | 
 198 |     |     /// @notice Accrue an underlying-denominated fee into the active credit index with a source tag.
 199 |     |     /// @dev Mirrors LibFeeIndex accrual using per-pool remainder tracking.
 200 |     |     function accrueWithSource(uint256 pid, uint256 amount, bytes32 source) internal {
 201 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 202 |     |         LibMaintenance.enforce(pid);
 203 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
 204 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
 205 |     |         _rollMatured(p);
 206 |     |         uint256 activeBase = p.activeCreditMaturedTotal;
 207 |     |         if (activeBase == 0) return;
 208 |     | 
 209 |     |         uint256 scaledAmount = Math.mulDiv(amount, INDEX_SCALE, 1);
 210 |     |         uint256 dividend = scaledAmount + p.activeCreditIndexRemainder;
 211 |     |         uint256 delta = dividend / activeBase;
 212 |     |         if (delta == 0) {
 213 |     |             p.activeCreditIndexRemainder = dividend;
 214 |     |             return;
 215 |     |         }
 216 |     | 
 217 |     |         p.activeCreditIndexRemainder = dividend - (delta * activeBase);
 218 |     |         uint256 newIndex = p.activeCreditIndex + delta;
 219 |     |         p.activeCreditIndex = newIndex;
 220 |     |         emit ActiveCreditIndexAccrued(pid, amount, delta, newIndex, source);
 221 |     |     }
 222 |     | 
 223 |     |     /// @notice Settle pending active credit yield for a user into accrued ledger and checkpoint index.
 224 |     |     function settle(uint256 pid, bytes32 user) internal {
 225 |     |         LibMaintenance.enforce(pid);
 226 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
 227 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
 228 |     |         _rollMatured(p);
 229 |     | 
 230 |     |         _settleState(p, p.userActiveCreditStateEncumbrance[user], pid, user);
 231 |     |         _settleState(p, p.userActiveCreditStateDebt[user], pid, user);
 232 |     |     }
 233 |     | 
 234 |     |     /// @notice View helper returning accrued + pending active credit yield for a user.
 235 |     |     function pendingYield(uint256 pid, bytes32 user) internal view returns (uint256) {
 236 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
 237 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
 238 |     | 
 239 |     |         uint256 amount = p.userAccruedYield[user];
 240 |     |         amount += _pendingForState(p, p.userActiveCreditStateEncumbrance[user]);
 241 |     |         amount += _pendingForState(p, p.userActiveCreditStateDebt[user]);
 242 |     |         return amount;
 243 |     |     }
 244 |     | 
 245 |     |     /// @notice View helper returning only active credit pending yield (excludes already accrued ledger).
 246 |     |     function pendingActiveCredit(uint256 pid, bytes32 user) internal view returns (uint256) {
 247 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
 248 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
 249 |     |         uint256 amount;
 250 |     |         amount += _pendingForState(p, p.userActiveCreditStateEncumbrance[user]);
 251 |     |         amount += _pendingForState(p, p.userActiveCreditStateDebt[user]);
 252 |     |         return amount;
 253 |     |     }
 254 |     | 
 255 |     |     function _pendingForState(Types.PoolData storage p, Types.ActiveCreditState storage state)
 256 |     |         private
 257 |     |         view
 258 |     |         returns (uint256)
 259 |     |     {
 260 |     |         if (!_isMature(state)) return 0;
 261 |     |         uint256 globalIndex = p.activeCreditIndex;
 262 |     |         uint256 prevIndex = state.indexSnapshot;
 263 |     |         if (globalIndex <= prevIndex || state.principal == 0) return 0;
 264 |     |         uint256 delta = globalIndex - prevIndex;
 265 |     |         return Math.mulDiv(state.principal, delta, INDEX_SCALE);
 266 |     |     }
 267 |     | 
 268 |     |     function _settleState(
 269 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 270 |     |         Types.ActiveCreditState storage state,
 271 |     |         uint256 pid,
 272 |     |         bytes32 user
 273 |     |     ) private {
 274 |     |         uint256 globalIndex = p.activeCreditIndex;
 275 |     |         uint256 prevIndex = state.indexSnapshot;
 276 |     | 
 277 |     |         if (state.principal == 0) {
 278 |     |             state.indexSnapshot = globalIndex;
 279 |     |             state.startTime = 0;
 280 |     |             return;
 281 |     |         }
 282 |     | 
 283 |     |         if (!_isMature(state)) {
 284 |     |             state.indexSnapshot = globalIndex;
 285 |     |             return;
 286 |     |         }
 287 |     | 
 288 |     |         if (globalIndex > prevIndex) {
 289 |     |             uint256 delta = globalIndex - prevIndex;
 290 |     |             uint256 added = Math.mulDiv(state.principal, delta, INDEX_SCALE);
 291 |     |             if (added > 0) {
 292 |     |                 p.userAccruedYield[user] += added;
 293 |     |                 emit ActiveCreditSettled(pid, user, prevIndex, globalIndex, added, p.userAccruedYield[user]);
 294 |     |             }
 295 |     |         }
 296 |     |         state.indexSnapshot = globalIndex;
 297 |     |     }
 298 |     | 
 299 |     |     function _rollMatured(Types.PoolData storage p) private {
 300 |     |         uint64 currentHour = _currentHour();
 301 |     |         uint64 storedStart = p.activeCreditPendingStartHour;
 302 |     |         if (storedStart == 0) {
 303 |     |             p.activeCreditPendingStartHour = currentHour + 1;
 304 |     |             p.activeCreditPendingCursor = 0;
 305 |     |             return;
 306 |     |         }
 307 |     |         uint64 startHour = storedStart - 1;
 308 |     |         if (currentHour <= startHour) return;
 309 |     | 
 310 |     |         uint64 elapsed = currentHour - startHour;
 311 |     |         if (elapsed >= BUCKET_COUNT) {
 312 |     |             for (uint8 i = 0; i < BUCKET_COUNT; i++) {
 313 |     |                 uint256 pending = p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[i];
 314 |     |                 if (pending > 0) {
 315 |     |                     p.activeCreditMaturedTotal += pending;
 316 |     |                     p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[i] = 0;
 317 |     |                 }
 318 |     |             }
 319 |     |             p.activeCreditPendingStartHour = currentHour + 1;
 320 |     |             p.activeCreditPendingCursor = 0;
 321 |     |             return;
 322 |     |         }
 323 |     | 
 324 |     |         uint8 cursor = p.activeCreditPendingCursor;
 325 |     |         for (uint64 i = 0; i < elapsed; i++) {
 326 |     |             uint256 pending = p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[cursor];
 327 |     |             if (pending > 0) {
 328 |     |                 p.activeCreditMaturedTotal += pending;
 329 |     |                 p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[cursor] = 0;
 330 |     |             }
 331 |     |             cursor = uint8((cursor + 1) % BUCKET_COUNT);
 332 |     |         }
 333 |     |         p.activeCreditPendingCursor = cursor;
 334 |     |         p.activeCreditPendingStartHour = startHour + elapsed + 1;
 335 |     |     }
 336 |     | 
 337 |     |     function _scheduleState(Types.PoolData storage p, Types.ActiveCreditState storage state) private {
 338 |     |         uint256 principal = state.principal;
 339 |     |         if (principal == 0) return;
 340 |     |         _rollMatured(p);
 341 |     |         if (_isMature(state)) {
 342 |     |             p.activeCreditMaturedTotal += principal;
 343 |     |             return;
 344 |     |         }
 345 |     |         uint64 maturityHour = _maturityHour(state.startTime);
 346 |     |         uint64 startHour = p.activeCreditPendingStartHour - 1;
 347 |     |         if (maturityHour <= startHour) {
 348 |     |             p.activeCreditMaturedTotal += principal;
 349 |     |             return;
 350 |     |         }
 351 |     |         uint64 offset = maturityHour - startHour - 1;
 352 |     |         if (offset >= BUCKET_COUNT) {
 353 |     |             uint8 last = uint8((p.activeCreditPendingCursor + (BUCKET_COUNT - 1)) % BUCKET_COUNT);
 354 |     |             p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[last] += principal;
 355 |     |             return;
 356 |     |         }
 357 |     |         uint8 index = uint8((p.activeCreditPendingCursor + uint8(offset)) % BUCKET_COUNT);
 358 |     |         p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[index] += principal;
 359 |     |     }
 360 |     | 
 361 |     |     function _removeFromBase(Types.PoolData storage p, Types.ActiveCreditState storage state, uint256 amount) private {
 362 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 363 |     |         _rollMatured(p);
 364 |     |         if (_isMature(state)) {
 365 |     |             if (p.activeCreditMaturedTotal >= amount) {
 366 |     |                 p.activeCreditMaturedTotal -= amount;
 367 |     |             } else {
 368 |     |                 p.activeCreditMaturedTotal = 0;
 369 |     |             }
 370 |     |             return;
 371 |     |         }
 372 |     |         uint64 maturityHour = _maturityHour(state.startTime);
 373 |     |         uint64 startHour = p.activeCreditPendingStartHour - 1;
 374 |     |         if (maturityHour <= startHour) {
 375 |     |             if (p.activeCreditMaturedTotal >= amount) {
 376 |     |                 p.activeCreditMaturedTotal -= amount;
 377 |     |             } else {
 378 |     |                 p.activeCreditMaturedTotal = 0;
 379 |     |             }
 380 |     |             return;
 381 |     |         }
 382 |     |         uint64 offset = maturityHour - startHour - 1;
 383 |     |         if (offset >= BUCKET_COUNT) {
 384 |     |             if (p.activeCreditMaturedTotal >= amount) {
 385 |     |                 p.activeCreditMaturedTotal -= amount;
 386 |     |             } else {
 387 |     |                 p.activeCreditMaturedTotal = 0;
 388 |     |             }
 389 |     |             return;
 390 |     |         }
 391 |     |         uint8 index = uint8((p.activeCreditPendingCursor + uint8(offset)) % BUCKET_COUNT);
 392 |     |         uint256 bucket = p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[index];
 393 |     |         if (bucket >= amount) {
 394 |     |             p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[index] = bucket - amount;
 395 |     |             return;
 396 |     |         }
 397 |     |         p.activeCreditPendingBuckets[index] = 0;
 398 |     |         uint256 remainder = amount - bucket;
 399 |     |         if (p.activeCreditMaturedTotal >= remainder) {
 400 |     |             p.activeCreditMaturedTotal -= remainder;
 401 |     |         } else {
 402 |     |             p.activeCreditMaturedTotal = 0;
 403 |     |         }
 404 |     |     }
 405 |     | 
 406 |     |     function _currentHour() private view returns (uint64) {
 407 |     |         return uint64(block.timestamp / BUCKET_SIZE);
 408 |     |     }
 409 |     | 
 410 |     |     function _maturityHour(uint40 startTime) private pure returns (uint64) {
 411 |     |         return uint64((uint256(startTime) + TIME_GATE) / BUCKET_SIZE);
 412 |     |     }
 413 |     | 
 414 |     |     function _isMature(Types.ActiveCreditState storage state) private view returns (bool) {
 415 |     |         if (state.principal == 0) return false;
 416 |     |         if (block.timestamp <= state.startTime) return false;
 417 |     |         uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - state.startTime;
 418 |     |         return elapsed >= TIME_GATE;
 419 |     |     }
 420 |     | }
 421 |     |

  59 |     |         assembly {

  61 |     |         }
  62 |     |     }
  63 |     | 
  64 |     |     /// @notice Return timelock, honoring historical slot layout used in tests/scripts.
  65 |     |     function timelockAddress(AppStorage storage store) internal view returns (address tl) {
  66 |     |         tl = store.timelock;
  67 |     |         if (tl != address(0)) {
  68 |     |             return tl;
  69 |     |         }
  70 |     |         bytes32 slot = APP_STORAGE_POSITION;
  71 |     |         assembly {
  72 |     |             tl := sload(add(slot, 8))
  73 |     |         }
  74 |     |         if (tl != address(0)) {
  75 |     |             return tl;
  76 |     |         }
  77 |     |         assembly {
  78 |     |             tl := shr(8, sload(add(slot, 3)))
  79 |     |         }
  80 |     |     }
  81 |     | 
  82 |     |     /// @notice Return index creation fee with backward-compatible slot lookup.
  83 |     |     function indexCreationFee(AppStorage storage store) internal view returns (uint256 fee) {
  84 |     |         fee = store.indexCreationFee;
  85 |     |         if (fee != 0) {
  86 |     |             return fee;
  87 |     |         }
  88 |     |         bytes32 slot = APP_STORAGE_POSITION;
  89 |     |         assembly {
  90 |     |             fee := sload(add(slot, 9))
  91 |     |         }
  92 |     |     }
  93 |     | 
  94 |     |     /// @notice Return treasury, honoring historical slot layout used in tests/scripts.
  95 |     |     function treasuryAddress(AppStorage storage store) internal view returns (address treasury) {
  96 |     |         treasury = store.treasury;
  97 |     |         if (treasury != address(0)) {
  98 |     |             return treasury;
  99 |     |         }
 100 |     |         if (store.defaultPoolConfigSet) {
 101 |     |             return treasury;
 102 |     |         }
 103 |     |         bytes32 slot = APP_STORAGE_POSITION;
 104 |     |         assembly {
 105 |     |             treasury := sload(add(slot, 4))
 106 |     |         }
 107 |     |     }
 108 |     | 
 109 |     |     function treasurySplitBps(AppStorage storage store) internal view returns (uint16) {
 110 |     |         return store.treasuryShareConfigured ? store.treasuryShareBps : DEFAULT_TREASURY_SHARE_BPS;
 111 |     |     }

 115 |     |     }

  9 |     | /// @notice Unified currency helper for native ETH and ERC20 operations.
 10 |     | library LibCurrency {

 12 |     | 
 13 |     |     function isNative(address token) internal pure returns (bool) {
 14 | *   |         return token == address(0);
 15 |     |     }
 16 |     | 
 17 | *   |     function assertZeroMsgValue() internal view {
 18 | *   |         if (msg.value != 0) {
 19 | *r  |             revert UnexpectedMsgValue(msg.value);
 20 |     |         }
 21 |     |     }
 22 |     | 
 23 |     |     function assertMsgValue(address token, uint256 amount) internal view {
 24 |     |         if (isNative(token)) {
 25 |     |             if (msg.value != 0 && msg.value != amount) {
 26 |     |                 revert UnexpectedMsgValue(msg.value);
 27 |     |             }
 28 |     |             return;
 29 |     |         }
 30 |     |         if (msg.value != 0) {
 31 |     |             revert UnexpectedMsgValue(msg.value);
 32 |     |         }
 33 |     |     }
 34 |     | 
 35 |     |     function balanceOfSelf(address token) internal view returns (uint256) {
 36 |     |         if (isNative(token)) {
 37 |     |             return address(this).balance;
 38 |     |         }
 39 |     |         return IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
 40 |     |     }
 41 |     | 
 42 |     |     function nativeAvailable() internal view returns (uint256) {
 43 |     |         uint256 balance = address(this).balance;
 44 |     |         uint256 tracked = LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal;
 45 |     |         return balance > tracked ? balance - tracked : 0;
 46 |     |     }
 47 |     | 
 48 | *   |     function pull(address token, address from, uint256 amount) internal returns (uint256 received) {
 49 | *   |         if (isNative(token)) {
 50 |     |             if (amount == 0) {
 51 |     |                 return 0;
 52 |     |             }
 53 |     |             if (msg.value > 0) {
 54 |     |                 if (msg.value != amount) {
 55 |     |                     revert UnexpectedMsgValue(msg.value);
 56 |     |                 }
 57 |     |                 LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal += amount;
 58 |     |                 return amount;
 59 |     |             }
 60 |     |             uint256 available = nativeAvailable();
 61 |     |             if (amount > available) {
 62 |     |                 revert InsufficientPoolLiquidity(amount, available);
 63 |     |             }
 64 |     |             LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal += amount;
 65 |     |             return amount;
 66 |     |         }
 67 |     | 
 68 | *   |         if (amount == 0) {
 69 |     |             return 0;
 70 |     |         }
 71 | *   |         uint256 balanceBefore = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
 72 |     |         IERC20(token).safeTransferFrom(from, address(this), amount);
 73 | *   |         uint256 balanceAfter = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
 74 | *   |         received = balanceAfter - balanceBefore;
 75 |     |     }
 76 |     | 
 77 |     |     function transfer(address token, address to, uint256 amount) internal {
 78 |     |         if (amount == 0) {
 79 |     |             return;
 80 |     |         }
 81 |     |         if (isNative(token)) {
 82 |     |             (bool success,) = to.call{value: amount}("");
 83 |     |             if (!success) {
 84 |     |                 revert NativeTransferFailed(to, amount);
 85 |     |             }
 86 |     |             return;
 87 |     |         }
 88 |     |         IERC20(token).safeTransfer(to, amount);
 89 |     |     }

 194 |     |     /// @notice Determine whether a position has any outstanding direct or rolling offers.
 195 |     |     function hasOutstandingOffers(bytes32 positionKey) internal view returns (bool) {
 196 |     |         DirectTypes.DirectStorage storage ds = directStorage();
 197 |     |         (, , uint256 lenderOfferCount) = ds.lenderOffers.meta(positionKey);
 198 |     |         (, , uint256 ratioLenderOfferCount) = ds.ratioLenderOffers.meta(positionKey);
 199 |     |         (, , uint256 borrowerOfferCount) = ds.borrowerOffersByPosition.meta(positionKey);
 200 |     |         (, , uint256 rollingLenderOfferCount) = ds.rollingLenderOffers.meta(positionKey);
 201 |     |         (, , uint256 rollingBorrowerOfferCount) = ds.rollingBorrowerOffersByPosition.meta(positionKey);
 202 |     |         (, , uint256 ratioBorrowerOfferCount) = ds.ratioBorrowerOffers.meta(positionKey);
 203 |     |         return lenderOfferCount > 0
 204 |     |             || ratioLenderOfferCount > 0
 205 |     |             || borrowerOfferCount > 0
 206 |     |             || rollingLenderOfferCount > 0
 207 |     |             || rollingBorrowerOfferCount > 0
 208 |     |             || ratioBorrowerOfferCount > 0;
 209 |     |     }

 284 |     | 
 285 |     |     /// @notice Cancel all outstanding lender and borrower offers for a position (used on NFT transfer).
 286 |     |     function cancelOffersForPosition(bytes32 positionKey) internal {
 287 |     |         DirectTypes.DirectStorage storage ds = directStorage();
 288 |     |         (uint256[] memory lenderList, ) = ds.lenderOffers.page(positionKey, 0, 0);
 289 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < lenderList.length; i++) {
 290 |     |             uint256 offerId = lenderList[i];
 291 |     |             untrackLenderOffer(ds, positionKey, offerId);
 292 |     |             DirectTypes.DirectOffer storage offer = ds.offers[offerId];
 293 |     |             if (offer.lenderPositionId == 0 || offer.cancelled || offer.filled) {
 294 |     |                 continue;
 295 |     |             }
 296 |     |             offer.cancelled = true;
 297 |     |             Types.PoolData storage lenderPool = LibAppStorage.s().pools[offer.lenderPoolId];
 298 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.settle(offer.lenderPoolId, positionKey);
 299 |     |             LibEncumbrance.Encumbrance storage enc = LibEncumbrance.position(positionKey, offer.lenderPoolId);
 300 |     |             uint256 encBefore = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.lenderPoolId);
 301 |     |             uint256 escrowed = enc.directOfferEscrow;
 302 |     |             uint256 release = offer.isTranche ? ds.trancheRemaining[offerId] : offer.principal;
 303 |     |             if (release > escrowed) {
 304 |     |                 release = escrowed;
 305 |     |             }
 306 |     |             enc.directOfferEscrow = escrowed - release;
 307 |     |             uint256 encAfter = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.lenderPoolId);
 308 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.applyEncumbranceDelta(
 309 |     |                 lenderPool, offer.lenderPoolId, positionKey, encBefore, encAfter
 310 |     |             );
 311 |     |             if (offer.isTranche) {
 312 |     |                 ds.trancheRemaining[offerId] = 0;
 313 |     |             }
 314 |     |         }
 315 |     | 
 316 |     |         (uint256[] memory borrowerList, ) = ds.borrowerOffersByPosition.page(positionKey, 0, 0);
 317 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < borrowerList.length; i++) {
 318 |     |             uint256 offerId = borrowerList[i];
 319 |     |             untrackBorrowerOffer(ds, positionKey, offerId);
 320 |     |             DirectTypes.DirectBorrowerOffer storage offer = ds.borrowerOffers[offerId];
 321 |     |             if (offer.borrowerPositionId == 0 || offer.cancelled || offer.filled) {
 322 |     |                 continue;
 323 |     |             }
 324 |     |             offer.cancelled = true;
 325 |     |             Types.PoolData storage collateralPool = LibAppStorage.s().pools[offer.collateralPoolId];
 326 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.settle(offer.collateralPoolId, positionKey);
 327 |     |             LibEncumbrance.Encumbrance storage enc = LibEncumbrance.position(positionKey, offer.collateralPoolId);
 328 |     |             uint256 encBefore = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.collateralPoolId);
 329 |     |             uint256 locked = enc.directLocked;
 330 |     |             if (locked >= offer.collateralLockAmount) {
 331 |     |                 enc.directLocked = locked - offer.collateralLockAmount;
 332 |     |             } else {
 333 |     |                 enc.directLocked = 0;
 334 |     |             }
 335 |     |             uint256 encAfter = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.collateralPoolId);
 336 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.applyEncumbranceDelta(
 337 |     |                 collateralPool, offer.collateralPoolId, positionKey, encBefore, encAfter
 338 |     |             );
 339 |     |         }
 340 |     | 
 341 |     |         (uint256[] memory ratioList, ) = ds.ratioLenderOffers.page(positionKey, 0, 0);
 342 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < ratioList.length; i++) {
 343 |     |             uint256 offerId = ratioList[i];
 344 |     |             untrackRatioLenderOffer(ds, positionKey, offerId);
 345 |     |             DirectTypes.DirectRatioTrancheOffer storage offer = ds.ratioOffers[offerId];
 346 |     |             if (offer.lenderPositionId == 0 || offer.cancelled || offer.filled) {
 347 |     |                 continue;
 348 |     |             }
 349 |     |             offer.cancelled = true;
 350 |     |             offer.filled = true;
 351 |     |             Types.PoolData storage lenderPool = LibAppStorage.s().pools[offer.lenderPoolId];
 352 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.settle(offer.lenderPoolId, positionKey);
 353 |     |             LibEncumbrance.Encumbrance storage enc = LibEncumbrance.position(positionKey, offer.lenderPoolId);
 354 |     |             uint256 encBefore = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.lenderPoolId);
 355 |     |             uint256 escrowed = enc.directOfferEscrow;
 356 |     |             uint256 release = offer.principalRemaining;
 357 |     |             if (release > escrowed) {
 358 |     |                 release = escrowed;
 359 |     |             }
 360 |     |             offer.principalRemaining = 0;
 361 |     |             enc.directOfferEscrow = escrowed - release;
 362 |     |             uint256 encAfter = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.lenderPoolId);
 363 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.applyEncumbranceDelta(
 364 |     |                 lenderPool, offer.lenderPoolId, positionKey, encBefore, encAfter
 365 |     |             );
 366 |     |         }
 367 |     | 
 368 |     |         // Cancel borrower ratio tranche offers
 369 |     |         (uint256[] memory borrowerRatioList, ) = ds.ratioBorrowerOffers.page(positionKey, 0, 0);
 370 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < borrowerRatioList.length; i++) {
 371 |     |             uint256 offerId = borrowerRatioList[i];
 372 |     |             untrackRatioBorrowerOffer(ds, positionKey, offerId);
 373 |     |             DirectTypes.DirectBorrowerRatioTrancheOffer storage offer = ds.borrowerRatioOffers[offerId];
 374 |     |             if (offer.borrowerPositionId == 0 || offer.cancelled || offer.filled) {
 375 |     |                 continue;
 376 |     |             }
 377 |     |             offer.cancelled = true;
 378 |     |             offer.filled = true;
 379 |     |             Types.PoolData storage collateralPool = LibAppStorage.s().pools[offer.collateralPoolId];
 380 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.settle(offer.collateralPoolId, positionKey);
 381 |     |             LibEncumbrance.Encumbrance storage enc = LibEncumbrance.position(positionKey, offer.collateralPoolId);
 382 |     |             uint256 encBefore = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.collateralPoolId);
 383 |     |             uint256 locked = enc.directLocked;
 384 |     |             uint256 release = offer.collateralRemaining;
 385 |     |             if (release > locked) {
 386 |     |                 release = locked;
 387 |     |             }
 388 |     |             offer.collateralRemaining = 0;
 389 |     |             enc.directLocked = locked - release;
 390 |     |             uint256 encAfter = LibEncumbrance.totalForActiveCredit(positionKey, offer.collateralPoolId);
 391 |     |             LibActiveCreditIndex.applyEncumbranceDelta(
 392 |     |                 collateralPool, offer.collateralPoolId, positionKey, encBefore, encAfter
 393 |     |             );
 394 |     |         }
 395 |     | 
 396 |     |     }
 397 |     | }

/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibEncumbrance.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import {EncumbranceUnderflow} from "./Errors.sol";
   5 |     | 
   6 |     | /// @notice Central storage and helpers for all encumbrance components per position and pool.
   7 |     | library LibEncumbrance {
   8 |     |     bytes32 internal constant STORAGE_POSITION = keccak256("equallend.encumbrance.storage");
   9 |     | 
  10 |     |     struct Encumbrance {
  11 |     |         uint256 directLocked;
  12 |     |         uint256 directLent;
  13 |     |         uint256 directOfferEscrow;
  14 |     |         uint256 indexEncumbered;
  15 |     |     }
  16 |     | 
  17 |     |     struct EncumbranceStorage {
  18 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => mapping(uint256 => Encumbrance)) encumbrance;
  19 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => mapping(uint256 => mapping(uint256 => uint256))) encumberedByIndex;
  20 |     |     }
  21 |     | 
  22 |     |     event EncumbranceIncreased(
  23 |     |         bytes32 indexed positionKey,
  24 |     |         uint256 indexed poolId,
  25 |     |         uint256 indexed indexId,
  26 |     |         uint256 amount,
  27 |     |         uint256 totalEncumbered,
  28 |     |         uint256 indexEncumbered
  29 |     |     );
  30 |     |     event EncumbranceDecreased(
  31 |     |         bytes32 indexed positionKey,
  32 |     |         uint256 indexed poolId,
  33 |     |         uint256 indexed indexId,
  34 |     |         uint256 amount,
  35 |     |         uint256 totalEncumbered,
  36 |     |         uint256 indexEncumbered
  37 |     |     );
  38 |     | 
  39 |     |     function s() internal pure returns (EncumbranceStorage storage es) {
  40 |     |         bytes32 storagePosition = STORAGE_POSITION;
  41 |     |         assembly {
  42 |     |             es.slot := storagePosition
  43 |     |         }
  44 |     |     }
  45 |     | 
  46 |     |     function position(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId) internal view returns (Encumbrance storage enc) {
  47 |     |         enc = s().encumbrance[positionKey][poolId];
  48 |     |     }
  49 |     | 
  50 |     |     function get(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId) internal view returns (Encumbrance memory enc) {
  51 |     |         enc = s().encumbrance[positionKey][poolId];
  52 |     |     }
  53 |     | 
  54 |     |     function total(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId) internal view returns (uint256) {
  55 |     |         Encumbrance storage enc = s().encumbrance[positionKey][poolId];
  56 |     |         return enc.directLocked + enc.directLent + enc.directOfferEscrow + enc.indexEncumbered;
  57 |     |     }
  58 |     | 
  59 |     |     function totalForActiveCredit(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId) internal view returns (uint256) {
  60 |     |         Encumbrance storage enc = s().encumbrance[positionKey][poolId];
  61 |     |         return enc.directLocked + enc.directLent + enc.directOfferEscrow;
  62 |     |     }
  63 |     | 
  64 |     |     function getIndexEncumbered(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId) internal view returns (uint256) {
  65 |     |         return s().encumbrance[positionKey][poolId].indexEncumbered;
  66 |     |     }
  67 |     | 
  68 |     |     function getIndexEncumberedForIndex(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId, uint256 indexId)
  69 |     |         internal
  70 |     |         view
  71 |     |         returns (uint256)
  72 |     |     {
  73 |     |         return s().encumberedByIndex[positionKey][poolId][indexId];
  74 |     |     }
  75 |     | 
  76 |     |     function encumberIndex(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId, uint256 indexId, uint256 amount) internal {
  77 |     |         EncumbranceStorage storage es = s();
  78 |     |         Encumbrance storage enc = es.encumbrance[positionKey][poolId];
  79 |     |         uint256 newTotal = enc.indexEncumbered + amount;
  80 |     |         enc.indexEncumbered = newTotal;
  81 |     |         uint256 newIndexTotal = es.encumberedByIndex[positionKey][poolId][indexId] + amount;
  82 |     |         es.encumberedByIndex[positionKey][poolId][indexId] = newIndexTotal;
  83 |     |         emit EncumbranceIncreased(positionKey, poolId, indexId, amount, newTotal, newIndexTotal);
  84 |     |     }
  85 |     | 
  86 |     |     function unencumberIndex(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 poolId, uint256 indexId, uint256 amount) internal {
  87 |     |         EncumbranceStorage storage es = s();
  88 |     |         Encumbrance storage enc = es.encumbrance[positionKey][poolId];
  89 |     |         uint256 currentIndex = es.encumberedByIndex[positionKey][poolId][indexId];
  90 |     |         if (amount > currentIndex) {
  91 |     |             revert EncumbranceUnderflow(amount, currentIndex);
  92 |     |         }
  93 |     |         uint256 currentTotal = enc.indexEncumbered;
  94 |     |         if (amount > currentTotal) {
  95 |     |             revert EncumbranceUnderflow(amount, currentTotal);
  96 |     |         }
  97 |     |         uint256 newTotal = currentTotal - amount;
  98 |     |         uint256 newIndexTotal = currentIndex - amount;
  99 |     |         enc.indexEncumbered = newTotal;
 100 |     |         es.encumberedByIndex[positionKey][poolId][indexId] = newIndexTotal;
 101 |     |         emit EncumbranceDecreased(positionKey, poolId, indexId, amount, newTotal, newIndexTotal);
 102 |     |     }
 103 |     | }
 104 |     |

  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;

  4 |     | import {Types} from "./Types.sol";
  5 |     | import "./Errors.sol";

  8 |     | /// @notice EqualIndex storage, events, and errors.
  9 |     | library LibEqualIndex {
 10 |     |     bytes32 internal constant EQUAL_INDEX_STORAGE_POSITION = keccak256("equal.index.storage");
 11 | *r  |     uint256 internal constant INDEX_SCALE = 1e18;
 12 |     | 
 13 |     |     struct Index {
 14 |     |         address[] assets;
 15 |     |         uint256[] bundleAmounts;
 16 |     |         uint256 flashFeeBps;
 17 |     |         uint256 totalUnits;
 18 |     |         address token;
 19 |     |         bool paused;
 20 |     |         uint256 lastFeeAccrual; // Timestamp of last AUM fee accrual
 21 |     |     }
 22 |     | 
 23 |     |     struct EqualIndexStorage {
 24 |     |         uint256 indexCount;
 25 |     |         mapping(uint256 => Index) indexes;
 26 |     |         mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => uint256)) vaultBalances; // indexId -> asset -> balance
 27 |     |         bool flashReentrancy;
 28 |     |         address protocolFeeReceiver;
 29 |     |         mapping(uint256 => mapping(bytes32 => Types.ActionFeeConfig)) actionFees;
 30 |     |         address foundationAddress; // AUM fee recipient
 31 |     |         uint256 annualFeeBps; // Annual fee rate in basis points (100 = 1%)
 32 |     |     }
 33 |     | 
 34 |     |     event IndexCreated(
 35 |     |         uint256 indexed indexId,
 36 |     |         address token,
 37 |     |         address[] assets,
 38 |     |         uint256[] bundleAmounts,
 39 |     |         uint256 flashFeeBps
 40 |     |     );
 41 |     |     event Minted(uint256 indexed indexId, address indexed user, uint256 units, uint256[] assetsIn);
 42 |     |     event Burned(uint256 indexed indexId, address indexed user, uint256 units, uint256[] assetsOut);
 43 |     |     event FlashLoaned(
 44 |     |         uint256 indexed indexId,
 45 |     |         address indexed receiver,
 46 |     |         uint256 units,
 47 |     |         uint256[] loanAmounts,
 48 |     |         uint256[] fees
 49 |     |     );
 50 |     |     event Paused(uint256 indexed indexId, bool paused);
 51 |     |     event ProtocolFeeReceiverUpdated(address indexed newReceiver);
 52 |     |     event FoundationAddressUpdated(address indexed oldAddress, address indexed newAddress);
 53 |     |     event AnnualFeeBpsUpdated(uint256 oldFeeBps, uint256 newFeeBps);
 54 |     |     event AumFeeAccrued(uint256 indexed indexId, uint256 feeShares, uint256 timestamp);
 55 |     | 
 56 |     |     function s() internal pure returns (EqualIndexStorage storage es) {
 57 |     |         bytes32 position = EQUAL_INDEX_STORAGE_POSITION;
 58 |     |         assembly {
 59 |     |             es.slot := position
 60 |     |         }
 61 |     |     }
 62 |     | 
 63 |     |     function calcRequired(uint256 units, uint256[] storage bundle) internal view returns (uint256[] memory out) {
 64 |     |         uint256 len = bundle.length;
 65 |     |         out = new uint256[](len);
 66 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {
 67 |     |             out[i] = Math.mulDiv(bundle[i], units, INDEX_SCALE);
 68 |     |         }
 69 |     |     }
 70 |     | }
