/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibFeeIndex.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import {LibAppStorage} from "./LibAppStorage.sol";
   5 |     | import {LibMaintenance} from "./LibMaintenance.sol";
   6 |     | import {Types} from "./Types.sol";
   7 |     | import {LibNetEquity} from "./LibNetEquity.sol";
   8 |     | import {LibSolvencyChecks} from "./LibSolvencyChecks.sol";
   9 |     | import {InsufficientPoolLiquidity} from "./Errors.sol";
  10 |     | import {Math} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol";
  11 |     | 
  12 |     | /// @notice Fee index accounting over internal principal ledger (1e18 scale)
  13 |     | library LibFeeIndex {
  14 |     |     uint256 internal constant INDEX_SCALE = 1e18;
  15 |     | 
  16 |     |     event FeeIndexAccrued(uint256 indexed pid, uint256 amount, uint256 delta, uint256 newIndex, bytes32 source);
  17 |     |     event YieldSettled(
  18 |     |         uint256 indexed pid,
  19 |     |         bytes32 indexed user,
  20 |     |         uint256 prevIndex,
  21 |     |         uint256 newIndex,
  22 |     |         uint256 addedYield,
  23 |     |         uint256 totalAccruedYield
  24 |     |     );
  25 |     | 
  26 |     |     /// @notice Fee base for same-asset domains (netted by debt).
  27 |     |     function calculateFeeBaseSameAsset(uint256 principal, uint256 sameAssetDebt) internal pure returns (uint256) {
  28 |     |         return LibNetEquity.calculateFeeBaseSameAsset(principal, sameAssetDebt);
  29 |     |     }
  30 |     | 
  31 |     |     /// @notice Fee base for cross-asset domains (locked collateral + unlocked principal).
  32 |     |     function calculateFeeBaseCrossAsset(uint256 lockedCollateral, uint256 unlockedPrincipal)
  33 |     |         internal
  34 |     |         pure
  35 |     |         returns (uint256)
  36 |     |     {
  37 |     |         return LibNetEquity.calculateFeeBaseCrossAsset(lockedCollateral, unlockedPrincipal);
  38 |     |     }
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     /// @notice Fee base for a P2P borrower based on asset relationship.
  41 |     |     function calculateP2PBorrowerFeeBase(
  42 |     |         uint256 lockedCollateral,
  43 |     |         uint256 unlockedPrincipal,
  44 |     |         uint256 sameAssetDebt,
  45 |     |         bool isSameAsset
  46 |     |     ) internal pure returns (uint256 feeBase) {
  47 |     |         return LibNetEquity.calculateP2PBorrowerFeeBase(
  48 |     |             lockedCollateral,
  49 |     |             unlockedPrincipal,
  50 |     |             sameAssetDebt,
  51 |     |             isSameAsset
  52 |     |         );
  53 |     |     }
  54 |     | 
  55 |     |     /// @notice Accrue an underlying-denominated fee into the pool fee index with a source tag.
  56 |     |     /// @dev No-op if amount is zero or totalDeposits is zero; never decreases index.
  57 |     |     function accrueWithSource(uint256 pid, uint256 amount, bytes32 source) internal {
  58 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
  59 |     |         LibMaintenance.enforce(pid);
  60 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
  61 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
  62 |     |         uint256 totalDeposits = p.totalDeposits;
  63 |     |         if (totalDeposits == 0) return;
  64 |     |         uint256 reserved = p.totalDeposits + p.yieldReserve;
  65 |     |         // Count outstanding credit as backing for fee accrual to avoid blocking when funds are lent out.
  66 |     |         uint256 backing = p.trackedBalance + p.activeCreditPrincipalTotal;
  67 |     |         uint256 available = backing > reserved ? backing - reserved : 0;
  68 |     |         if (amount > available) {
  69 |     |             revert InsufficientPoolLiquidity(amount, available);
  70 |     |         }
  71 |     |         p.yieldReserve += amount;
  72 |     |         uint256 scaledAmount = Math.mulDiv(amount, INDEX_SCALE, 1);
  73 |     |         // Use per-pool remainder instead of global
  74 |     |         uint256 dividend = scaledAmount + p.feeIndexRemainder;
  75 |     |         uint256 delta = dividend / totalDeposits;
  76 |     |         if (delta == 0) {
  77 |     |             p.feeIndexRemainder = dividend;
  78 |     |             return;
  79 |     |         }
  80 |     |         p.feeIndexRemainder = dividend - (delta * totalDeposits);
  81 |     |         uint256 newIndex = p.feeIndex + delta;
  82 |     |         p.feeIndex = newIndex;
  83 |     |         emit FeeIndexAccrued(pid, amount, delta, newIndex, source);
  84 |     |     }
  85 |     | 
  86 |     |     /// @notice Accrue fee index with additional temporary backing (e.g., encumbered auction reserves).
  87 |     |     function accrueWithSourceUsingBacking(uint256 pid, uint256 amount, bytes32 source, uint256 extraBacking)
  88 |     |         internal
  89 |     |     {
  90 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
  91 |     |         LibMaintenance.enforce(pid);
  92 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
  93 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
  94 |     |         uint256 totalDeposits = p.totalDeposits;
  95 |     |         if (totalDeposits == 0) return;
  96 |     |         uint256 reserved = p.totalDeposits + p.yieldReserve;
  97 |     |         uint256 backing = p.trackedBalance + p.activeCreditPrincipalTotal + extraBacking;
  98 |     |         // Allow encumbered (flash) reserves to count toward available backing even when trackedBalance is unchanged.
  99 |     |         uint256 available = backing > reserved ? backing - reserved : extraBacking;
 100 |     |         if (amount > available) {
 101 |     |             revert InsufficientPoolLiquidity(amount, available);
 102 |     |         }
 103 |     |         p.yieldReserve += amount;
 104 |     |         uint256 scaledAmount = Math.mulDiv(amount, INDEX_SCALE, 1);
 105 |     |         uint256 dividend = scaledAmount + p.feeIndexRemainder;
 106 |     |         uint256 delta = dividend / totalDeposits;
 107 |     |         if (delta == 0) {
 108 |     |             p.feeIndexRemainder = dividend;
 109 |     |             return;
 110 |     |         }
 111 |     |         p.feeIndexRemainder = dividend - (delta * totalDeposits);
 112 |     |         uint256 newIndex = p.feeIndex + delta;
 113 |     |         p.feeIndex = newIndex;
 114 |     |         emit FeeIndexAccrued(pid, amount, delta, newIndex, source);
 115 |     |     }
 116 |     | 
 117 |     |     /// @notice Settle pending yield for a user into accrued ledger and checkpoint fee index.
 118 |     |     function settle(uint256 pid, bytes32 user) internal {
 119 |     |         LibMaintenance.enforce(pid);
 120 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
 121 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
 122 |     | 
 123 |     |         uint256 principal = p.userPrincipal[user];
 124 |     |         if (principal == 0) {
 125 |     |             // Update checkpoints even if no principal
 126 |     |             p.userFeeIndex[user] = p.feeIndex;
 127 |     |             p.userMaintenanceIndex[user] = p.maintenanceIndex;
 128 |     |             return;
 129 |     |         }
 130 |     | 
 131 |     |         // Apply maintenance fee reduction first (negative yield)
 132 |     |         uint256 globalMaintenanceIndex = p.maintenanceIndex;
 133 |     |         uint256 prevMaintenanceIndex = p.userMaintenanceIndex[user];
 134 |     |         if (globalMaintenanceIndex > prevMaintenanceIndex) {
 135 |     |             uint256 maintenanceDelta = globalMaintenanceIndex - prevMaintenanceIndex;
 136 |     |             uint256 maintenanceFee = Math.mulDiv(principal, maintenanceDelta, INDEX_SCALE);
 137 |     |             if (maintenanceFee > 0) {
 138 |     |                 if (maintenanceFee >= principal) {
 139 |     |                     principal = 0;
 140 |     |                     p.userPrincipal[user] = 0;
 141 |     |                 } else {
 142 |     |                     principal -= maintenanceFee;
 143 |     |                     p.userPrincipal[user] = principal;
 144 |     |                 }
 145 |     |             }
 146 |     |             p.userMaintenanceIndex[user] = globalMaintenanceIndex;
 147 |     |         }
 148 |     | 
 149 |     |         // Apply positive yield (after maintenance reduction)
 150 |     |         uint256 globalIndex = p.feeIndex;
 151 |     |         uint256 prevIndex = p.userFeeIndex[user];
 152 |     |         uint256 added;
 153 |     |         if (globalIndex > prevIndex && principal > 0) {
 154 |     |             uint256 delta = globalIndex - prevIndex;
 155 |     |             uint256 sameAssetDebt = LibSolvencyChecks.calculateSameAssetDebt(p, user, p.underlying);
 156 |     |             uint256 feeBase = LibNetEquity.calculateFeeBaseSameAsset(principal, sameAssetDebt);
 157 |     |             added = Math.mulDiv(feeBase, delta, INDEX_SCALE);
 158 |     |             if (added > 0) {
 159 |     |                 p.userAccruedYield[user] += added;
 160 |     |             }
 161 |     |         }
 162 |     |         p.userFeeIndex[user] = globalIndex;
 163 |     | 
 164 |     |         emit YieldSettled(pid, user, prevIndex, globalIndex, added, p.userAccruedYield[user]);
 165 |     |     }
 166 |     | 
 167 |     |     /// @notice View helper returning accrued + pending yield for a user.
 168 |     |     function pendingYield(uint256 pid, bytes32 user) internal view returns (uint256) {
 169 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
 170 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
 171 |     |         uint256 amount = p.userAccruedYield[user];
 172 |     |         uint256 principal = p.userPrincipal[user];
 173 |     | 
 174 |     |         if (principal == 0) return amount;
 175 |     | 
 176 |     |         // Apply pending maintenance fee reduction first
 177 |     |         uint256 globalMaintenanceIndex = p.maintenanceIndex;
 178 |     |         uint256 userMaintenanceIndex = p.userMaintenanceIndex[user];
 179 |     |         if (globalMaintenanceIndex > userMaintenanceIndex) {
 180 |     |             uint256 maintenanceDelta = globalMaintenanceIndex - userMaintenanceIndex;
 181 |     |             uint256 maintenanceFee = Math.mulDiv(principal, maintenanceDelta, INDEX_SCALE);
 182 |     |             if (maintenanceFee >= principal) {
 183 |     |                 principal = 0;
 184 |     |             } else {
 185 |     |                 principal -= maintenanceFee;
 186 |     |             }
 187 |     |         }
 188 |     | 
 189 |     |         // Apply pending positive yield (on reduced principal)
 190 |     |         uint256 globalIndex = p.feeIndex;
 191 |     |         uint256 userIndex = p.userFeeIndex[user];
 192 |     |         if (globalIndex > userIndex && principal > 0) {
 193 |     |             uint256 delta = globalIndex - userIndex;
 194 |     |             uint256 sameAssetDebt = LibSolvencyChecks.calculateSameAssetDebt(p, user, p.underlying);
 195 |     |             uint256 feeBase = LibNetEquity.calculateFeeBaseSameAsset(principal, sameAssetDebt);
 196 |     |             amount += Math.mulDiv(feeBase, delta, INDEX_SCALE);
 197 |     |         }
 198 |     | 
 199 |     |         return amount;
 200 |     |     }
 201 |     | }

/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibFeeRouter.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import {LibAppStorage} from "./LibAppStorage.sol";
   5 |     | import {LibFeeIndex} from "./LibFeeIndex.sol";
   6 |     | import {LibActiveCreditIndex} from "./LibActiveCreditIndex.sol";
   7 |     | import {LibCurrency} from "./LibCurrency.sol";
   8 |     | import {LibMaintenance} from "./LibMaintenance.sol";
   9 |     | import {Types} from "./Types.sol";
  10 |     | import {InsufficientPoolLiquidity, InsufficientPrincipal} from "./Errors.sol";
  11 |     | 
  12 |     | /// @notice Central fee router for ACI/FI/Treasury splits.
  13 |     | library LibFeeRouter {
  14 |     |     uint256 internal constant BPS_DENOMINATOR = 10_000;
  15 |     | 
  16 |     |     event ManagedPoolSystemShareRouted(
  17 |     |         uint256 indexed managedPid,
  18 |     |         uint256 indexed basePid,
  19 |     |         uint256 amount,
  20 |     |         bytes32 source
  21 |     |     );
  22 |     | 
  23 |     |     function previewSplit(uint256 amount)
  24 |     |         internal
  25 |     |         view
  26 |     |         returns (uint256 toTreasury, uint256 toActiveCredit, uint256 toFeeIndex)
  27 |     |     {
  28 |     |         if (amount == 0) return (0, 0, 0);
  29 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
  30 |     |         uint16 treasuryBps = LibAppStorage.treasurySplitBps(store);
  31 |     |         uint16 activeBps = LibAppStorage.activeCreditSplitBps(store);
  32 |     |         require(treasuryBps + activeBps <= BPS_DENOMINATOR, "FeeRouter: splits>100%");
  33 |     | 
  34 |     |         address treasury = LibAppStorage.treasuryAddress(store);
  35 |     |         toTreasury = treasury != address(0) ? (amount * treasuryBps) / BPS_DENOMINATOR : 0;
  36 |     |         toActiveCredit = (amount * activeBps) / BPS_DENOMINATOR;
  37 |     |         toFeeIndex = amount - toTreasury - toActiveCredit;
  38 |     |     }
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     /// @notice Route a fee amount into ACI/FI/Treasury for a single pool.
  41 |     |     /// @dev Use extraBacking when fee assets are encumbered (e.g., auction reserves).
  42 |     |     function routeSamePool(
  43 |     |         uint256 pid,
  44 |     |         uint256 amount,
  45 |     |         bytes32 source,
  46 |     |         bool pullFromTracked,
  47 |     |         uint256 extraBacking
  48 |     |     ) internal returns (uint256 toTreasury, uint256 toActiveCredit, uint256 toFeeIndex) {
  49 |     |         if (amount == 0) return (0, 0, 0);
  50 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
  51 |     |         Types.PoolData storage pool = store.pools[pid];
  52 |     | 
  53 |     |         (toTreasury, toActiveCredit, toFeeIndex) = previewSplit(amount);
  54 |     | 
  55 |     |         if (toTreasury > 0) {
  56 |     |             _transferTreasury(pool, toTreasury, pullFromTracked);
  57 |     |         }
  58 |     |         if (toActiveCredit > 0) {
  59 |     |             _accrueActiveCredit(pool, pid, toActiveCredit, source, extraBacking);
  60 |     |         }
  61 |     |         if (toFeeIndex > 0) {
  62 |     |             if (extraBacking > 0) {
  63 |     |                 LibFeeIndex.accrueWithSourceUsingBacking(pid, toFeeIndex, source, extraBacking);
  64 |     |             } else {
  65 |     |                 LibFeeIndex.accrueWithSource(pid, toFeeIndex, source);
  66 |     |             }
  67 |     |         }
  68 |     |     }
  69 |     | 
  70 |     |     /// @notice Route a fee amount with managed pool system share logic.
  71 |     |     /// @dev When pool is unmanaged, this is equivalent to routeSamePool.
  72 |     |     function routeManagedShare(
  73 |     |         uint256 pid,
  74 |     |         uint256 amount,
  75 |     |         bytes32 source,
  76 |     |         bool pullFromTracked,
  77 |     |         uint256 extraBacking
  78 |     |     ) internal returns (uint256 toTreasury, uint256 toActiveCredit, uint256 toFeeIndex) {
  79 |     |         if (amount == 0) return (0, 0, 0);
  80 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
  81 |     |         Types.PoolData storage pool = store.pools[pid];
  82 |     | 
  83 |     |         if (!pool.isManagedPool) {
  84 |     |             return routeSamePool(pid, amount, source, pullFromTracked, extraBacking);
  85 |     |         }
  86 |     | 
  87 |     |         uint16 systemShareBps = LibAppStorage.managedPoolSystemShareBps(store);
  88 |     |         if (systemShareBps == 0) {
  89 |     |             return routeSamePool(pid, amount, source, pullFromTracked, extraBacking);
  90 |     |         }
  91 |     | 
  92 |     |         uint256 systemShare = (amount * systemShareBps) / BPS_DENOMINATOR;
  93 |     |         uint256 managedShare = amount - systemShare;
  94 |     | 
  95 |     |         uint256 systemExtraBacking = (extraBacking * systemShareBps) / BPS_DENOMINATOR;
  96 |     |         uint256 managedExtraBacking = extraBacking - systemExtraBacking;
  97 |     | 
  98 |     |         if (systemShare > 0) {
  99 |     |             (uint256 sysTreasury, uint256 sysActive, uint256 sysFee) =
 100 |     |                 _routeSystemShare(store, pool, pid, systemShare, source, pullFromTracked, systemExtraBacking);
 101 |     |             toTreasury += sysTreasury;
 102 |     |             toActiveCredit += sysActive;
 103 |     |             toFeeIndex += sysFee;
 104 |     |         }
 105 |     | 
 106 |     |         if (managedShare > 0) {
 107 |     |             (uint256 manTreasury, uint256 manActive, uint256 manFee) =
 108 |     |                 routeSamePool(pid, managedShare, source, pullFromTracked, managedExtraBacking);
 109 |     |             toTreasury += manTreasury;
 110 |     |             toActiveCredit += manActive;
 111 |     |             toFeeIndex += manFee;
 112 |     |         }
 113 |     |     }
 114 |     | 
 115 |     |     /// @notice Accrue active credit yield with yieldReserve backing.
 116 |     |     function accrueActiveCredit(uint256 pid, uint256 amount, bytes32 source, uint256 extraBacking) internal {
 117 |     |         Types.PoolData storage pool = LibAppStorage.s().pools[pid];
 118 |     |         _accrueActiveCredit(pool, pid, amount, source, extraBacking);
 119 |     |     }
 120 |     | 
 121 |     |     function _routeSystemShare(
 122 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store,
 123 |     |         Types.PoolData storage managedPool,
 124 |     |         uint256 managedPid,
 125 |     |         uint256 amount,
 126 |     |         bytes32 source,
 127 |     |         bool pullFromTracked,
 128 |     |         uint256 extraBacking
 129 |     |     ) private returns (uint256 toTreasury, uint256 toActiveCredit, uint256 toFeeIndex) {
 130 |     |         uint256 basePid = store.assetToPoolId[managedPool.underlying];
 131 |     |         Types.PoolData storage basePool = store.pools[basePid];
 132 |     |         if (basePid == 0 || !basePool.initialized || basePool.totalDeposits == 0) {
 133 |     |             _routeSystemShareToTreasury(managedPool, amount, pullFromTracked);
 134 |     |             emit ManagedPoolSystemShareRouted(managedPid, 0, amount, source);
 135 |     |             return (amount, 0, 0);
 136 |     |         }
 137 |     | 
 138 |     |         _transferBacking(managedPool, basePool, amount);
 139 |     |         (toTreasury, toActiveCredit, toFeeIndex) = routeSamePool(basePid, amount, source, true, extraBacking);
 140 |     |         emit ManagedPoolSystemShareRouted(managedPid, basePid, amount, source);
 141 |     |     }
 142 |     | 
 143 |     |     function _transferBacking(Types.PoolData storage fromPool, Types.PoolData storage toPool, uint256 amount)
 144 |     |         private
 145 |     |     {
 146 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 147 |     |         uint256 tracked = fromPool.trackedBalance;
 148 |     |         if (tracked < amount) {
 149 |     |             revert InsufficientPrincipal(amount, tracked);
 150 |     |         }
 151 |     |         fromPool.trackedBalance = tracked - amount;
 152 |     |         toPool.trackedBalance += amount;
 153 |     |     }
 154 |     | 
 155 |     |     function _routeSystemShareToTreasury(Types.PoolData storage pool, uint256 amount, bool pullFromTracked)
 156 |     |         private
 157 |     |     {
 158 |     |         address treasury = LibAppStorage.treasuryAddress(LibAppStorage.s());
 159 |     |         if (treasury == address(0) || amount == 0) return;
 160 |     |         uint256 contractBal = LibCurrency.balanceOfSelf(pool.underlying);
 161 |     |         if (contractBal < amount) {
 162 |     |             revert InsufficientPrincipal(amount, contractBal);
 163 |     |         }
 164 |     |         if (pullFromTracked) {
 165 |     |             uint256 tracked = pool.trackedBalance;
 166 |     |             if (tracked < amount) {
 167 |     |                 revert InsufficientPrincipal(amount, tracked);
 168 |     |             }
 169 |     |             pool.trackedBalance = tracked - amount;
 170 |     |             if (LibCurrency.isNative(pool.underlying)) {
 171 |     |                 LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal -= amount;
 172 |     |             }
 173 |     |         }
 174 |     |         LibCurrency.transfer(pool.underlying, treasury, amount);
 175 |     |     }
 176 |     | 
 177 |     |     function _transferTreasury(Types.PoolData storage pool, uint256 amount, bool pullFromTracked) private {
 178 |     |         address treasury = LibAppStorage.treasuryAddress(LibAppStorage.s());
 179 |     |         if (treasury == address(0) || amount == 0) return;
 180 |     |         uint256 contractBal = LibCurrency.balanceOfSelf(pool.underlying);
 181 |     |         if (contractBal < amount) {
 182 |     |             revert InsufficientPrincipal(amount, contractBal);
 183 |     |         }
 184 |     |         if (pullFromTracked) {
 185 |     |             uint256 tracked = pool.trackedBalance;
 186 |     |             if (tracked < amount) {
 187 |     |                 revert InsufficientPrincipal(amount, tracked);
 188 |     |             }
 189 |     |             pool.trackedBalance = tracked - amount;
 190 |     |             if (LibCurrency.isNative(pool.underlying)) {
 191 |     |                 LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal -= amount;
 192 |     |             }
 193 |     |         }
 194 |     |         LibCurrency.transfer(pool.underlying, treasury, amount);
 195 |     |     }
 196 |     | 
 197 |     |     function _accrueActiveCredit(
 198 |     |         Types.PoolData storage pool,
 199 |     |         uint256 pid,
 200 |     |         uint256 amount,
 201 |     |         bytes32 source,
 202 |     |         uint256 extraBacking
 203 |     |     ) private {
 204 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 205 |     |         _reserveYield(pool, pid, amount, extraBacking);
 206 |     |         LibActiveCreditIndex.accrueWithSource(pid, amount, source);
 207 |     |     }
 208 |     | 
 209 |     |     function _reserveYield(
 210 |     |         Types.PoolData storage pool,
 211 |     |         uint256 pid,
 212 |     |         uint256 amount,
 213 |     |         uint256 extraBacking
 214 |     |     ) private {
 215 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 216 |     |         LibMaintenance.enforce(pid);
 217 |     |         uint256 reserved = pool.totalDeposits + pool.yieldReserve;
 218 |     |         uint256 backing = pool.trackedBalance + pool.activeCreditPrincipalTotal + extraBacking;
 219 |     |         uint256 available = backing > reserved ? backing - reserved : extraBacking;
 220 |     |         if (amount > available) {
 221 |     |             revert InsufficientPoolLiquidity(amount, available);
 222 |     |         }
 223 |     |         pool.yieldReserve += amount;
 224 |     |     }
 225 |     | }

/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibLoanManager.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | /// @notice Library for efficient per-position fixed-term loan management.
   5 |     | /// @dev Implements a doubly-linked list per position for O(1) insertions and deletions by loanId.
   6 |     | library LibLoanManager {
   7 |     |     struct LoanNode {
   8 |     |         uint256 prev;
   9 |     |         uint256 next;
  10 |     |         bool exists;
  11 |     |     }
  12 |     | 
  13 |     |     struct LoanList {
  14 |     |         uint256 head;
  15 |     |         uint256 tail;
  16 |     |         uint256 length;
  17 |     |         mapping(uint256 => LoanNode) loanNodes;
  18 |     |     }
  19 |     | 
  20 |     |     struct LoanManager {
  21 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => LoanList) lists;
  22 |     |     }
  23 |     | 
  24 |     |     /// @notice Add a loan to the end of a position's list.
  25 |     |     /// @dev Reverts if the loanId is already present for this position.
  26 |     |     function addLoan(
  27 |     |         LoanManager storage self,
  28 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
  29 |     |         uint256 loanId
  30 |     |     ) internal {
  31 |     |         require(loanId != 0, "LoanManager: loanId=0");
  32 |     |         LoanList storage list = self.lists[positionKey];
  33 |     |         LoanNode storage node = list.loanNodes[loanId];
  34 |     |         require(!node.exists, "LoanManager: exists");
  35 |     | 
  36 |     |         node.exists = true;
  37 |     |         uint256 tailId = list.tail;
  38 |     |         if (tailId == 0) {
  39 |     |             // First node in list
  40 |     |             list.head = loanId;
  41 |     |             list.tail = loanId;
  42 |     |         } else {
  43 |     |             LoanNode storage tailNode = list.loanNodes[tailId];
  44 |     |             tailNode.next = loanId;
  45 |     |             node.prev = tailId;
  46 |     |             list.tail = loanId;
  47 |     |         }
  48 |     |         list.length += 1;
  49 |     |     }
  50 |     | 
  51 |     |     /// @notice Remove a loan from a position's list.
  52 |     |     /// @dev Reverts if the loanId is not present for this position.
  53 |     |     function removeLoan(
  54 |     |         LoanManager storage self,
  55 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
  56 |     |         uint256 loanId
  57 |     |     ) internal {
  58 |     |         LoanList storage list = self.lists[positionKey];
  59 |     |         LoanNode storage node = list.loanNodes[loanId];
  60 |     |         require(node.exists, "LoanManager: missing");
  61 |     | 
  62 |     |         uint256 prevId = node.prev;
  63 |     |         uint256 nextId = node.next;
  64 |     | 
  65 |     |         if (prevId == 0) {
  66 |     |             // Removing head
  67 |     |             list.head = nextId;
  68 |     |         } else {
  69 |     |             list.loanNodes[prevId].next = nextId;
  70 |     |         }
  71 |     | 
  72 |     |         if (nextId == 0) {
  73 |     |             // Removing tail
  74 |     |             list.tail = prevId;
  75 |     |         } else {
  76 |     |             list.loanNodes[nextId].prev = prevId;
  77 |     |         }
  78 |     | 
  79 |     |         delete list.loanNodes[loanId];
  80 |     |         list.length -= 1;
  81 |     |     }
  82 |     | 
  83 |     |     /// @notice Get paginated loans for a position.
  84 |     |     /// @param positionKey Position identifier (e.g. positionKey for a Position NFT).
  85 |     |     /// @param offset Starting index in the list (0-based).
  86 |     |     /// @param limit Maximum number of loans to return (0 = until end).
  87 |     |     /// @return loanIds Array of loan IDs for the requested slice.
  88 |     |     /// @return total Total number of loans for this position.
  89 |     |     function getLoansByPosition(
  90 |     |         LoanManager storage self,
  91 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
  92 |     |         uint256 offset,
  93 |     |         uint256 limit
  94 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256[] memory loanIds, uint256 total) {
  95 |     |         LoanList storage list = self.lists[positionKey];
  96 |     |         total = list.length;
  97 |     |         if (offset >= total) {
  98 |     |             return (new uint256[](0), total);
  99 |     |         }
 100 |     | 
 101 |     |         uint256 remaining = total - offset;
 102 |     |         if (limit == 0 || limit > remaining) {
 103 |     |             limit = remaining;
 104 |     |         }
 105 |     | 
 106 |     |         loanIds = new uint256[](limit);
 107 |     |         uint256 current = list.head;
 108 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < offset && current != 0; i++) {
 109 |     |             current = list.loanNodes[current].next;
 110 |     |         }
 111 |     | 
 112 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < limit && current != 0; i++) {
 113 |     |             loanIds[i] = current;
 114 |     |             current = list.loanNodes[current].next;
 115 |     |         }
 116 |     |     }
 117 |     | 
 118 |     |     /// @notice Return list metadata for a position.
 119 |     |     /// @dev Intended for testing and introspection.
 120 |     |     function getListMeta(
 121 |     |         LoanManager storage self,
 122 |     |         bytes32 positionKey
 123 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 head, uint256 tail, uint256 length) {
 124 |     |         LoanList storage list = self.lists[positionKey];
 125 |     |         return (list.head, list.tail, list.length);
 126 |     |     }
 127 |     | 
 128 |     |     /// @notice Return node links for a given loan.
 129 |     |     /// @dev Intended for testing and introspection.
 130 |     |     function getNode(
 131 |     |         LoanManager storage self,
 132 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
 133 |     |         uint256 loanId
 134 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 prev, uint256 next, bool exists) {
 135 |     |         LoanNode storage node = self.lists[positionKey].loanNodes[loanId];
 136 |     |         return (node.prev, node.next, node.exists);
 137 |     |     }
 138 |     | }
 139 |     | 

/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibMaintenance.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import {LibAppStorage} from "./LibAppStorage.sol";
   5 |     | import {LibCurrency} from "./LibCurrency.sol";
   6 |     | import {Types} from "./Types.sol";
   7 |     | 
   8 |     | /// @notice Helper for deterministic pool-level maintenance accrual and payouts.
   9 |     | library LibMaintenance {
  10 |     |     uint256 internal constant MAINTENANCE_EPOCH = 1 days;
  11 |     | 
  12 |     |     event MaintenanceCharged(
  13 |     |         uint256 indexed pid,
  14 |     |         address indexed receiver,
  15 |     |         uint256 epochsCharged,
  16 |     |         uint256 amountAccrued,
  17 |     |         uint256 amountPaid,
  18 |     |         uint256 outstanding
  19 |     |     );
  20 |     | 
  21 |     |     function enforce(uint256 pid) internal {
  22 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
  23 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
  24 |     |         if (!p.initialized) {
  25 |     |             return;
  26 |     |         }
  27 |     |         address receiver = store.foundationReceiver;
  28 |     |         if (receiver == address(0)) {
  29 |     |             return;
  30 |     |         }
  31 |     | 
  32 |     |         (uint256 amountAccrued, uint256 epochs) = _accrue(store, p);
  33 |     |         uint256 paid = _pay(p, receiver);
  34 |     | 
  35 |     |         if (amountAccrued > 0 || paid > 0) {
  36 |     |             emit MaintenanceCharged(pid, receiver, epochs, amountAccrued, paid, p.pendingMaintenance);
  37 |     |         }
  38 |     |     }
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     function forcePay(uint256 pid) internal {
  41 |     |         LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store = LibAppStorage.s();
  42 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = store.pools[pid];
  43 |     |         if (!p.initialized) return;
  44 |     |         address receiver = store.foundationReceiver;
  45 |     |         if (receiver == address(0)) return;
  46 |     |         uint256 paid = _pay(p, receiver);
  47 |     |         if (paid > 0) {
  48 |     |             emit MaintenanceCharged(pid, receiver, 0, 0, paid, p.pendingMaintenance);
  49 |     |         }
  50 |     |     }
  51 |     | 
  52 |     |     function _accrue(LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage store, Types.PoolData storage p)
  53 |     |         private
  54 |     |         returns (uint256 amountAccrued, uint256 epochs)
  55 |     |     {
  56 |     |         uint16 rateBps = p.poolConfig.maintenanceRateBps;
  57 |     |         if (rateBps == 0) {
  58 |     |             rateBps = store.defaultMaintenanceRateBps;
  59 |     |             if (rateBps == 0) {
  60 |     |                 rateBps = 100; // default 1% when unset
  61 |     |             }
  62 |     |         }
  63 |     | 
  64 |     |         uint64 lastTimestamp = p.lastMaintenanceTimestamp;
  65 |     |         uint64 nowTs = uint64(block.timestamp);
  66 |     |         if (lastTimestamp == 0) {
  67 |     |             p.lastMaintenanceTimestamp = nowTs;
  68 |     |             return (0, 0);
  69 |     |         }
  70 |     | 
  71 |     |         if (nowTs <= lastTimestamp) {
  72 |     |             return (0, 0);
  73 |     |         }
  74 |     | 
  75 |     |         uint256 elapsed = nowTs - lastTimestamp;
  76 |     |         epochs = elapsed / MAINTENANCE_EPOCH;
  77 |     |         if (epochs == 0) {
  78 |     |             return (0, 0);
  79 |     |         }
  80 |     | 
  81 |     |         p.lastMaintenanceTimestamp = lastTimestamp + uint64(epochs * MAINTENANCE_EPOCH);
  82 |     | 
  83 |     |         uint256 tvl = p.totalDeposits;
  84 |     |         if (tvl == 0) {
  85 |     |             return (0, epochs);
  86 |     |         }
  87 |     | 
  88 |     |         amountAccrued = (tvl * rateBps * epochs) / (365 * 10_000);
  89 |     |         if (amountAccrued == 0) {
  90 |     |             return (0, epochs);
  91 |     |         }
  92 |     | 
  93 |     |         // Reduce totalDeposits to reflect maintenance fee taken from depositors
  94 |     |         if (p.totalDeposits >= amountAccrued) {
  95 |     |             p.totalDeposits -= amountAccrued;
  96 |     |         } else {
  97 |     |             amountAccrued = p.totalDeposits;
  98 |     |             p.totalDeposits = 0;
  99 |     |         }
 100 |     | 
 101 |     |         // Apply negative fee index to proportionally reduce all user principals
 102 |     |         // This works like negative yield - reducing everyone's balance proportionally
 103 |     |         _applyMaintenanceToIndex(store, p, amountAccrued);
 104 |     | 
 105 |     |         p.pendingMaintenance += amountAccrued;
 106 |     |         return (amountAccrued, epochs);
 107 |     |     }
 108 |     | 
 109 |     |     function _applyMaintenanceToIndex(LibAppStorage.AppStorage storage, Types.PoolData storage p, uint256 amount)
 110 |     |         private
 111 |     |     {
 112 |     |         if (amount == 0) return;
 113 |     |         // Note: totalDeposits has already been reduced, so we use the OLD value for calculation
 114 |     |         uint256 oldTotal = p.totalDeposits + amount;
 115 |     |         if (oldTotal == 0) return;
 116 |     | 
 117 |     |         // Calculate the reduction ratio: amount / oldTotal
 118 |     |         // We'll reduce each user's principal by this ratio when they settle
 119 |     |         // Using the fee index mechanism: negative delta reduces principal
 120 |     |         uint256 scaledAmount = (amount * 1e18) / 1;
 121 |     |         // Use per-pool remainder instead of global
 122 |     |         uint256 dividend = scaledAmount + p.maintenanceIndexRemainder;
 123 |     |         uint256 delta = dividend / oldTotal;
 124 |     | 
 125 |     |         if (delta == 0) {
 126 |     |             p.maintenanceIndexRemainder = dividend;
 127 |     |             return;
 128 |     |         }
 129 |     | 
 130 |     |         p.maintenanceIndexRemainder = dividend - (delta * oldTotal);
 131 |     |         p.maintenanceIndex += delta;
 132 |     |     }
 133 |     | 
 134 |     |     function _pay(Types.PoolData storage p, address receiver) private returns (uint256 paid) {
 135 |     |         uint256 outstanding = p.pendingMaintenance;
 136 |     |         if (outstanding == 0) {
 137 |     |             return 0;
 138 |     |         }
 139 |     |         
 140 |     |         // Use tracked balance for pool isolation - prevents draining other pools
 141 |     |         uint256 poolAvailable = p.trackedBalance;
 142 |     |         uint256 contractBalance = LibCurrency.balanceOfSelf(p.underlying);
 143 |     |         
 144 |     |         // Can only pay up to the minimum of: outstanding, pool's tracked balance, and contract balance
 145 |     |         paid = outstanding;
 146 |     |         if (paid > poolAvailable) {
 147 |     |             paid = poolAvailable;
 148 |     |         }
 149 |     |         if (paid > contractBalance) {
 150 |     |             paid = contractBalance;
 151 |     |         }
 152 |     |         
 153 |     |         if (paid == 0) {
 154 |     |             return 0;
 155 |     |         }
 156 |     |         
 157 |     |         p.pendingMaintenance = outstanding - paid;
 158 |     |         if (p.trackedBalance >= paid) {
 159 |     |             p.trackedBalance -= paid; // Decrease tracked balance
 160 |     |         }
 161 |     |         if (LibCurrency.isNative(p.underlying)) {
 162 |     |             LibAppStorage.s().nativeTrackedTotal -= paid;
 163 |     |         }
 164 |     |         LibCurrency.transfer(p.underlying, receiver, paid);
 165 |     |         return paid;
 166 |     |     }
 167 |     | 
 168 |     |     function epochLength() internal pure returns (uint256) {
 169 |     |         return MAINTENANCE_EPOCH;
 170 |     |     }
 171 |     | }
 172 |     |

  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /// @notice Pure helpers for net equity and fee base calculations.
  5 |     | import {FeeBaseOverflow, InvalidAssetComparison, NegativeFeeBase} from "./Errors.sol";
  6 |     | 
  7 |     | library LibNetEquity {
  8 |     |     /// @notice Net equity for same-asset debt scenarios.
  9 |     |     function calculateNetEquity(uint256 principal, uint256 sameAssetDebt) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 10 |     |         if (sameAssetDebt >= principal) {
 11 |     |             return 0;
 12 |     |         }
 13 |     |         return principal - sameAssetDebt;
 14 |     |     }
 15 |     | 
 16 |     |     /// @notice Fee base for same-asset domains (netted by debt).
 17 |     |     function calculateFeeBaseSameAsset(uint256 principal, uint256 sameAssetDebt) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 18 |     |         return calculateNetEquity(principal, sameAssetDebt);
 19 |     |     }
 20 |     | 
 21 |     |     /// @notice Validate same-asset fee base and revert if debt exceeds principal.
 22 |     |     function validateFeeBaseSameAsset(uint256 principal, uint256 sameAssetDebt) internal pure returns (uint256) {
 23 |     |         if (sameAssetDebt > principal) {
 24 |     |             revert NegativeFeeBase();
 25 |     |         }
 26 |     |         return principal - sameAssetDebt;
 27 |     |     }
 28 |     | 
 29 |     |     /// @notice Fee base for cross-asset domains (locked collateral + unlocked principal).
 30 |     |     function calculateFeeBaseCrossAsset(uint256 lockedCollateral, uint256 unlockedPrincipal)
 31 |     |         internal
 32 |     |         pure
 33 |     |         returns (uint256)
 34 |     |     {
 35 |     |         if (lockedCollateral > type(uint256).max - unlockedPrincipal) {
 36 |     |             revert FeeBaseOverflow();
 37 |     |         }
 38 |     |         return lockedCollateral + unlockedPrincipal;
 39 |     |     }
 40 |     | 
 41 |     |     /// @notice Determine if a P2P loan is same-asset or cross-asset.
 42 |     |     function isSameAssetP2P(address collateralAsset, address lentAsset) internal pure returns (bool) {
 43 |     |         if (collateralAsset == address(0) || lentAsset == address(0)) {
 44 |     |             revert InvalidAssetComparison();
 45 |     |         }
 46 |     |         return collateralAsset == lentAsset;
 47 |     |     }
 48 |     | 
 49 |     |     /// @notice Fee base for a P2P borrower based on asset relationship.
 50 |     |     function calculateP2PBorrowerFeeBase(
 51 |     |         uint256 lockedCollateral,
 52 |     |         uint256 unlockedPrincipal,
 53 |     |         uint256 sameAssetDebt,
 54 |     |         bool isSameAsset
 55 |     |     ) internal pure returns (uint256 feeBase) {
 56 |     |         if (isSameAsset) {
 57 |     |             uint256 principal = calculateFeeBaseCrossAsset(lockedCollateral, unlockedPrincipal);
 58 |     |             return calculateFeeBaseSameAsset(principal, sameAssetDebt);
 59 |     |         }
 60 |     | 
 61 |     |         return calculateFeeBaseCrossAsset(lockedCollateral, unlockedPrincipal);
 62 |     |     }
 63 |     | }
 64 |     |

   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import {
   5 |     |     PoolMembershipRequired,
   6 |     |     MembershipAlreadyExists,
   7 |     |     CannotClearMembership,
   8 |     |     WhitelistRequired
   9 |     | } from "./Errors.sol";
  10 |     | import {LibAppStorage} from "./LibAppStorage.sol";
  11 |     | import {Types} from "./Types.sol";
  12 |     | import {LibDirectStorage} from "./LibDirectStorage.sol";
  13 |     | import {LibEncumbrance} from "./LibEncumbrance.sol";
  14 |     | 
  15 |     | /// @title LibPoolMembership
  16 |     | /// @notice Storage and helpers for managing position membership across pools
  17 |     | library LibPoolMembership {
  18 |     |     bytes32 internal constant POOL_MEMBERSHIP_STORAGE_POSITION =
  19 |     |         keccak256("equal.lend.pool.membership.storage");
  20 |     | 
  21 |     |     struct PoolMembershipStorage {
  22 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => mapping(uint256 => bool)) joined; // positionKey => poolId => joined
  23 |     |     }
  24 |     | 
  25 |     |     /// @notice Return pool membership storage
  26 |     |     function s() internal pure returns (PoolMembershipStorage storage ps) {
  27 |     |         bytes32 position = POOL_MEMBERSHIP_STORAGE_POSITION;
  28 |     |         assembly {
  29 |     |             ps.slot := position
  30 |     |         }
  31 |     |     }
  32 |     | 
  33 |     |     /// @notice Ensure a position is a member of the specified pool.
  34 |     |     /// @dev When allowAutoJoin is true, missing membership will be created; otherwise reverts.
  35 |     |     /// @return alreadyMember True if membership already existed before this call.
  36 |     |     function _ensurePoolMembership(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 pid, bool allowAutoJoin)
  37 |     |         internal
  38 |     |         returns (bool alreadyMember)
  39 |     |     {
  40 |     |         PoolMembershipStorage storage store = s();
  41 |     |         alreadyMember = store.joined[positionKey][pid];
  42 |     |         if (alreadyMember) {
  43 |     |             // Existing membership allows continued operations even if later removed from whitelist.
  44 |     |             return true;
  45 |     |         }
  46 |     | 
  47 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = LibAppStorage.s().pools[pid];
  48 |     |         if (p.isManagedPool && p.whitelistEnabled && !p.whitelist[positionKey]) {
  49 |     |             revert WhitelistRequired(positionKey, pid);
  50 |     |         }
  51 |     | 
  52 |     |         if (!allowAutoJoin) {
  53 |     |             revert PoolMembershipRequired(positionKey, pid);
  54 |     |         }
  55 |     |         store.joined[positionKey][pid] = true;
  56 |     |         return false;
  57 |     |     }
  58 |     | 
  59 |     |     /// @notice Explicitly join a pool for the given position key.
  60 |     |     function _joinPool(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 pid) internal {
  61 |     |         PoolMembershipStorage storage store = s();
  62 |     |         if (store.joined[positionKey][pid]) {
  63 |     |             revert MembershipAlreadyExists(positionKey, pid);
  64 |     |         }
  65 |     |         store.joined[positionKey][pid] = true;
  66 |     |     }
  67 |     | 
  68 |     |     /// @notice Clear membership for a position and pool combination.
  69 |     |     /// @dev Caller is responsible for ensuring balances and obligations are settled before clearing.
  70 |     |     function _leavePool(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 pid, bool canClear, string memory reason) internal {
  71 |     |         PoolMembershipStorage storage store = s();
  72 |     |         if (!store.joined[positionKey][pid]) {
  73 |     |             revert PoolMembershipRequired(positionKey, pid);
  74 |     |         }
  75 |     |         if (!canClear) {
  76 |     |             revert CannotClearMembership(positionKey, pid, reason);
  77 |     |         }
  78 |     |         delete store.joined[positionKey][pid];
  79 |     |     }
  80 |     | 
  81 |     |     /// @notice Check whether a position key is already a member of a pool.
  82 |     |     function isMember(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 pid) internal view returns (bool) {
  83 |     |         return s().joined[positionKey][pid];
  84 |     |     }
  85 |     | 
  86 |     |     /// @notice Determine if membership can be cleared based on balances and obligations.
  87 |     |     /// @dev Returns a reason string describing the first blocking condition.
  88 |     |     function canClearMembership(bytes32 positionKey, uint256 pid)
  89 |     |         internal
  90 |     |         view
  91 |     |         returns (bool canClear, string memory reason)
  92 |     |     {
  93 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p = LibAppStorage.s().pools[pid];
  94 |     |         if (p.userPrincipal[positionKey] > 0) {
  95 |     |             return (false, "principal>0");
  96 |     |         }
  97 |     |         if (p.activeFixedLoanCount[positionKey] > 0) {
  98 |     |             return (false, "active fixed loans");
  99 |     |         }
 100 |     |         Types.RollingCreditLoan storage loan = p.rollingLoans[positionKey];
 101 |     |         if (loan.active && loan.principalRemaining > 0) {
 102 |     |             return (false, "rolling loan active");
 103 |     |         }
 104 |     |         LibEncumbrance.Encumbrance memory enc = LibEncumbrance.get(positionKey, pid);
 105 |     |         if (enc.directLocked > 0) {
 106 |     |             return (false, "locked direct principal");
 107 |     |         }
 108 |     |         if (LibDirectStorage.directStorage().directBorrowedPrincipal[positionKey][pid] > 0) {
 109 |     |             return (false, "direct borrowed principal");
 110 |     |         }
 111 |     |         if (enc.directLent > 0) {
 112 |     |             return (false, "direct lent principal");
 113 |     |         }
 114 |     |         if (enc.directOfferEscrow > 0) {
 115 |     |             return (false, "direct offer escrow");
 116 |     |         }
 117 |     |         return (true, "");
 118 |     |     }
 119 |     | }
 120 |     |

  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import {LibLoanManager} from "./LibLoanManager.sol";
  5 |     | 
  6 |     | /// @notice Thin wrapper around LibLoanManager for generic position-scoped linked lists.
  7 |     | /// @dev Provides consistent naming for Direct offers/agreements tracking.
  8 |     | library LibPositionList {
  9 |     |     using LibLoanManager for LibLoanManager.LoanManager;
 10 |     | 
 11 |     |     struct List {
 12 |     |         LibLoanManager.LoanManager manager;
 13 |     |     }
 14 |     | 
 15 |     |     function add(List storage self, bytes32 positionKey, uint256 id) internal {
 16 |     |         self.manager.addLoan(positionKey, id);
 17 |     |     }
 18 |     | 
 19 |     |     function remove(List storage self, bytes32 positionKey, uint256 id) internal {
 20 |     |         self.manager.removeLoan(positionKey, id);
 21 |     |     }
 22 |     | 
 23 |     |     function page(
 24 |     |         List storage self,

  4 |     | /// @title LibPositionNFT
  5 |     | /// @notice Library for Position NFT storage and position key derivation
  6 |     | /// @dev Position keys enable reuse of existing PoolData mappings for NFT-based positions
  7 |     | library LibPositionNFT {
  8 |     |     bytes32 internal constant POSITION_NFT_STORAGE_POSITION = 
  9 |     |         keccak256("equal.lend.position.nft.storage");
 10 |     | 
 11 |     |     struct PositionNFTStorage {
 12 |     |         address positionNFTContract;
 13 |     |         bool nftModeEnabled;
 14 |     |     }
 15 |     | 
 16 |     |     /// @notice Get the Position NFT storage
 17 |     |     /// @return ds The Position NFT storage struct
 18 |     |     function s() internal pure returns (PositionNFTStorage storage ds) {
 19 |     |         bytes32 position = POSITION_NFT_STORAGE_POSITION;
 20 |     |         assembly {
 21 |     |             ds.slot := position
 22 |     |         }
 23 |     |     }
 24 |     | 
 25 |     |     /// @notice Derive a position key from NFT contract address and token ID
 26 |     |     /// @dev Uses keccak256 hash to create a deterministic address-like key
 27 |     |     /// @param nftContract The address of the Position NFT contract
 28 |     |     /// @param tokenId The token ID of the Position NFT
 29 |     |     /// @return The derived position key as a bytes32 hash
 30 |     |     function getPositionKey(address nftContract, uint256 tokenId) 
 31 |     |         internal 
 32 |     |         pure 
 33 |     |         returns (bytes32) 
 34 |     |     {
 35 |     |         return keccak256(abi.encodePacked(nftContract, tokenId));
 36 |     |     }
 37 |     | 
 38 |     |     /// @notice Check if an address is an NFT position key
 39 |     |     /// @dev This is a placeholder - actual implementation would require reverse mapping
 40 |     |     /// @param key The address to check
 41 |     |     /// @return True if the address is an NFT position key
 42 |     |     function isNFTPosition(bytes32 key) internal view returns (bool) {
 43 |     |         // This would require additional storage to track which keys are NFT positions
 44 |     |         // For now, we rely on the NFT contract to manage this
 45 |     |         return s().nftModeEnabled && key != bytes32(0);
 46 |     |     }
 47 |     | }

/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibReentrancyGuard.sol
  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
  2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | /// @notice Diamond-wide reentrancy guard stored in a dedicated slot.
  5 |     | library LibReentrancyGuard {
  6 |     |     error ReentrancyGuard_ReentrantCall();
  7 |     | 
  8 |     |     // keccak256("equallend.reentrancy.guard")
  9 |     |     bytes32 private constant STORAGE_SLOT = 0x8c5ddf4f4cc0c16e2ee35f5e5ae3d5c1d3a1c9fbb0290c3044da811790970d65;
 10 |     |     uint256 private constant _NOT_ENTERED = 1;
 11 | *   |     uint256 private constant _ENTERED = 2;
 12 |     | 
 13 |     |     struct Layout {
 14 |     |         uint256 status;
 15 |     |     }
 16 |     | 
 17 |     |     function layout() private pure returns (Layout storage l) {
 18 |     |         bytes32 slot = STORAGE_SLOT;
 19 |     |         assembly {
 20 |     |             l.slot := slot
 21 |     |         }
 22 |     |     }
 23 |     | 
 24 | *   |     function _enter() internal {
 25 |     |         Layout storage l = layout();
 26 | *   |         if (l.status == _ENTERED) revert ReentrancyGuard_ReentrantCall();
 27 |     |         l.status = _ENTERED;
 28 |     |     }
 29 |     | 
 30 |     |     function _exit() internal {
 31 |     |         layout().status = _NOT_ENTERED;
 32 |     |     }
 33 |     | }
 34 |     | 
 35 |     | /// @notice Modifier helper that uses the shared guard storage.
 36 |     | abstract contract ReentrancyGuardModifiers {
 37 |     |     modifier nonReentrant() {
 38 | *   |         LibReentrancyGuard._enter();
 39 |     |         _;
 40 |     |         LibReentrancyGuard._exit();
 41 |     |     }
 42 |     | }
 43 |     | 

/home/hooftly/Projects/EqualFi/src/libraries/LibSolvencyChecks.sol
   1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1
   2 |     | pragma solidity ^0.8.20;
   3 |     | 
   4 |     | import {LibDirectStorage} from "./LibDirectStorage.sol";
   5 |     | import {DirectTypes} from "./DirectTypes.sol";
   6 |     | import {Types} from "./Types.sol";
   7 |     | import {LibNetEquity} from "./LibNetEquity.sol";
   8 |     | import {SameAssetDebtMismatch} from "./Errors.sol";
   9 |     | import {LibPositionHelpers} from "./LibPositionHelpers.sol";
  10 |     | import {LibEncumbrance} from "./LibEncumbrance.sol";
  11 |     | 
  12 |     | /// @title LibSolvencyChecks
  13 |     | /// @notice Shared utilities for deterministic solvency and debt calculations
  14 |     | library LibSolvencyChecks {
  15 |     |     /// @notice Check solvency for a position using only on-chain deterministic data
  16 |     |     /// @dev This function ensures NRF compliance by using only immutable pool config and on-chain state
  17 |     |     /// @param p The pool data storage reference
  18 |     |     /// @param positionKey The position key to check
  19 |     |     /// @param newPrincipal The principal amount after the proposed operation
  20 |     |     /// @param newDebt The total debt after the proposed operation
  21 |     |     /// @return isSolvent True if the position is solvent
  22 |     |     function checkSolvency(
  23 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
  24 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
  25 |     |         uint256 newPrincipal,
  26 |     |         uint256 newDebt
  27 |     |     ) internal view returns (bool isSolvent) {
  28 |     |         // Parameter retained for consistent signature; unused in deterministic check
  29 |     |         positionKey;
  30 |     |         // If no debt, position is always solvent
  31 |     |         if (newDebt == 0) {
  32 |     |             return true;
  33 |     |         }
  34 |     | 
  35 |     |         // Calculate max borrowable using ONLY immutable pool config (no oracles).
  36 |     |         // LTV must be set to a non-zero value; zero disables borrowing.
  37 |     |         uint16 ltvBps = p.poolConfig.depositorLTVBps;
  38 |     |         if (ltvBps == 0) {
  39 |     |             return false;
  40 |     |         }
  41 |     |         uint256 maxBorrowable = (newPrincipal * ltvBps) / 10_000;
  42 |     | 
  43 |     |         // Position is solvent if debt does not exceed max borrowable
  44 |     |         return newDebt <= maxBorrowable;
  45 |     |     }
  46 |     | 
  47 |     |     /// @notice Calculate current loan debts (rolling + fixed-term) for a position
  48 |     |     function calculateLoanDebts(
  49 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
  50 |     |         bytes32 positionKey
  51 |     |     )
  52 |     |         internal
  53 |     |         view
  54 |     |         returns (uint256 rollingDebt, uint256 fixedDebt, uint256 totalLoanDebt)
  55 |     |     {
  56 |     |         // Rolling loan debt
  57 |     |         Types.RollingCreditLoan storage rolling = p.rollingLoans[positionKey];
  58 |     |         if (rolling.active) {
  59 |     |             rollingDebt = rolling.principalRemaining;
  60 |     |         }
  61 |     | 
  62 |     |         // Fixed-term loan debt (cached aggregate)
  63 |     |         fixedDebt = p.fixedTermPrincipalRemaining[positionKey];
  64 |     | 
  65 |     |         totalLoanDebt = rollingDebt + fixedDebt;
  66 |     |     }
  67 |     | 
  68 |     |     /// @notice Calculate current loan debts (rolling + fixed-term) for a positionId.
  69 |     |     function calculateLoanDebtsById(
  70 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
  71 |     |         uint256 positionId
  72 |     |     )
  73 |     |         internal
  74 |     |         view
  75 |     |         returns (uint256 rollingDebt, uint256 fixedDebt, uint256 totalLoanDebt)
  76 |     |     {
  77 |     |         bytes32 positionKey = LibPositionHelpers.positionKey(positionId);
  78 |     |         return calculateLoanDebts(p, positionKey);
  79 |     |     }
  80 |     | 
  81 |     |     /// @notice Calculate same-asset debt for fee base calculations.
  82 |     |     function calculateSameAssetDebt(
  83 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
  84 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
  85 |     |         address poolAsset
  86 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 sameAssetDebt) {
  87 |     |         (uint256 rollingDebt, uint256 fixedDebt,) = calculateLoanDebts(p, positionKey);
  88 |     |         DirectTypes.DirectStorage storage ds = LibDirectStorage.directStorage();
  89 |     |         sameAssetDebt = rollingDebt + fixedDebt + ds.directSameAssetDebt[positionKey][poolAsset];
  90 |     |     }
  91 |     | 
  92 |     |     /// @notice Calculate same-asset debt for fee base calculations by positionId.
  93 |     |     function calculateSameAssetDebtById(
  94 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
  95 |     |         uint256 positionId,
  96 |     |         address poolAsset
  97 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 sameAssetDebt) {
  98 |     |         bytes32 positionKey = LibPositionHelpers.positionKey(positionId);
  99 |     |         return calculateSameAssetDebt(p, positionKey, poolAsset);
 100 |     |     }
 101 |     | 
 102 |     |     /// @notice Calculate same-asset and cross-asset debt totals by positionId.
 103 |     |     function calculateDebtByAsset(
 104 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 105 |     |         uint256 positionId,
 106 |     |         address poolAsset
 107 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 sameAssetDebt, uint256 crossAssetDebt, uint256 totalDebt) {
 108 |     |         bytes32 positionKey = LibPositionHelpers.positionKey(positionId);
 109 |     |         (uint256 rollingDebt, uint256 fixedDebt,) = calculateLoanDebts(p, positionKey);
 110 |     |         DirectTypes.DirectStorage storage ds = LibDirectStorage.directStorage();
 111 |     |         sameAssetDebt = rollingDebt + fixedDebt + ds.directSameAssetDebt[positionKey][poolAsset];
 112 |     | 
 113 |     |         (uint256[] memory agreements,) = LibDirectStorage.borrowerAgreementsPage(ds, positionKey, 0, 0);
 114 |     |         for (uint256 i = 0; i < agreements.length; i++) {
 115 |     |             DirectTypes.DirectAgreement storage agreement = ds.agreements[agreements[i]];
 116 |     |             if (agreement.status != DirectTypes.DirectStatus.Active) {
 117 |     |                 continue;
 118 |     |             }
 119 |     |             if (agreement.borrowAsset != poolAsset) {
 120 |     |                 crossAssetDebt += agreement.principal;
 121 |     |             }
 122 |     |         }
 123 |     | 
 124 |     |         totalDebt = sameAssetDebt + crossAssetDebt;
 125 |     |     }
 126 |     | 
 127 |     |     /// @notice Calculate total debt for a position using only on-chain data
 128 |     |     /// @dev Returns the sum of all active loan principals (rolling + fixed-term + direct)
 129 |     |     function calculateTotalDebt(
 130 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 131 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
 132 |     |         uint256 pid
 133 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 totalDebt) {
 134 |     |         (,, uint256 loanDebt) = calculateLoanDebts(p, positionKey);
 135 |     |         DirectTypes.DirectStorage storage ds = LibDirectStorage.directStorage();
 136 |     |         uint256 directDebt = ds.directBorrowedPrincipal[positionKey][pid];
 137 |     |         // Direct locks reduce withdrawable collateral but are not debt; only include active borrowed principal.
 138 |     |         totalDebt = loanDebt + directDebt;
 139 |     |     }
 140 |     | 
 141 |     |     /// @notice Calculate withdrawable principal after direct locks and offer escrow.
 142 |     |     function calculateWithdrawablePrincipal(
 143 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 144 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
 145 |     |         uint256 pid
 146 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 withdrawable) {
 147 |     |         return calculateAvailablePrincipal(p, positionKey, pid);
 148 |     |     }
 149 |     | 
 150 |     |     /// @notice Calculate available principal after direct locks, offer escrow, and index encumbrance.
 151 |     |     function calculateAvailablePrincipal(
 152 |     |         Types.PoolData storage p,
 153 |     |         bytes32 positionKey,
 154 |     |         uint256 pid
 155 |     |     ) internal view returns (uint256 available) {
 156 |     |         uint256 principal = p.userPrincipal[positionKey];
 157 |     |         LibEncumbrance.Encumbrance memory enc = LibEncumbrance.get(positionKey, pid);
 158 |     |         uint256 totalEncumbered =
 159 |     |             enc.directLocked + enc.directLent + enc.directOfferEscrow + enc.indexEncumbered;
 160 |     |         if (totalEncumbered >= principal) {
 161 |     |             return 0;
 162 |     |         }
 163 |     |         available = principal - totalEncumbered;
 164 |     |     }
 165 |     | 
 166 |     |     /// @notice Fee base invariant for same-asset domains.
 167 |     |     function checkFeeBaseInvariant(uint256 principal, uint256 sameAssetDebt) internal pure returns (bool) {
 168 |     |         uint256 feeBase = LibNetEquity.calculateFeeBaseSameAsset(principal, sameAssetDebt);
 169 |     |         if (sameAssetDebt >= principal) {
 170 |     |             return feeBase == 0;
 171 |     |         }
 172 |     |         return feeBase <= principal - sameAssetDebt;
 173 |     |     }
 174 |     | 
 175 |     |     /// @notice Validate debt snapshot consistency.
 176 |     |     function validateSameAssetDebt(uint256 expected, uint256 actual) internal pure {
 177 |     |         if (expected != actual) {
 178 |     |             revert SameAssetDebtMismatch(expected, actual);
 179 |     |         }
 180 |     |     }
 181 |     | }
 182 |     |

 178 |     |     struct PoolData {
 179 |     |         // Core identity
 180 |     |         address underlying;
 181 |     |         bool initialized;
 182 |     |         
 183 |     |         // Pool configuration (stored once, never modified)
 184 |     |         PoolConfig poolConfig;
 185 |     |

 187 |     |         uint16 currentAumFeeBps;
 188 |     |

 190 |     |         bool deprecated;
 191 |     |

 193 |     |         uint256 totalDeposits;

 196 |     |         uint64 lastMaintenanceTimestamp;

 198 |     |         uint256 nextFixedLoanId;
 199 |     |         uint256 userCount;                  // Total number of users with deposits in this pool
 200 |     |         uint256 feeIndexRemainder;          // Per-pool remainder for fee index precision
 201 |     |         uint256 maintenanceIndexRemainder;  // Per-pool remainder for maintenance index precision
 202 |     |         uint256 yieldReserve;               // Backing reserve for accrued yield claims
 203 |     |         uint256 activeCreditIndex;          // Active credit index (parallel to feeIndex)
 204 |     |         uint256 activeCreditIndexRemainder; // Remainder for active credit index precision
 205 |     |         uint256 activeCreditPrincipalTotal; // Sum of active credit principal across debt/encumbrance states
 206 |     |         uint256 activeCreditMaturedTotal;   // Matured principal base for active credit accruals
 207 |     |         uint64 activeCreditPendingStartHour; // Last processed hour for pending principal buckets
 208 |     |         uint8 activeCreditPendingCursor;     // Ring cursor for pending principal buckets
 209 |     |         uint256[24] activeCreditPendingBuckets; // Pending principal scheduled to mature
 210 |     |         uint256 trackedBalance;             // Per-pool tracked token balance for isolation
 211 |     | 
 212 |     |         // Managed pool state
 213 |     |         bool isManagedPool;
 214 |     |         address manager;
 215 |     |         ManagedPoolConfig managedConfig;
 216 |     |         bool whitelistEnabled;
 217 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => bool) whitelist;
 218 |     |         
 219 |     |         // ─── Per-user ledger ───────────────────────────────
 220 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) userPrincipal;
 221 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) userFeeIndex;
 222 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) userMaintenanceIndex;
 223 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) userAccruedYield;
 224 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) externalCollateral;
 225 |     |         // ─── Debt tracking (positionId) ───────────────────
 226 |     |         mapping(uint256 => uint256) sameAssetDebt;
 227 |     |         mapping(uint256 => uint256) crossAssetDebt;
 228 |     |         // ─── Fee base tracking (positionId) ───────────────
 229 |     |         mapping(uint256 => uint256) feeBaseCheckpoint;
 230 |     |         mapping(uint256 => uint256) lastFeeBase;
 231 |     |         // ─── Loan state and indexes ────────────────────────
 232 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => RollingCreditLoan) rollingLoans;
 233 |     |         mapping(uint256 => FixedTermLoan) fixedTermLoans;
 234 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) activeFixedLoanCount;
 235 |     |         /// @notice Cached sum of principalRemaining across all fixed-term loans for a position
 236 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256) fixedTermPrincipalRemaining;
 237 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => uint256[]) userFixedLoanIds;
 238 |     |         /// @notice Mapping from positionKey => loanId => index in userFixedLoanIds[positionKey]
 239 |     |         /// @dev Enables O(1) loan removal without array scans when used by index-aware helpers.
 240 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => mapping(uint256 => uint256)) loanIdToIndex;
 241 |     |         // ─── Action fee configuration ──────────────────────
 242 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => ActionFeeConfig) actionFees;
 243 |     |         // ─── Active Credit state (positionKey) ─────────────
 244 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => ActiveCreditState) userActiveCreditStateEncumbrance;
 245 |     |         mapping(bytes32 => ActiveCreditState) userActiveCreditStateDebt;
 246 |     |     }
 247 |     | 
 248 |     |     struct ActiveCreditState {
 249 |     |         uint256 principal;
 250 |     |         uint40 startTime;
 251 |     |         uint256 indexSnapshot;
 252 |     |     }
 253 |     | }

   9 |     | /// @notice Interface for direct-offer hooks from the Diamond (cancellation/checks)

  12 |     |     function hasOpenOffers(bytes32 positionKey) external view returns (bool);
  13 |     | }

  17 |     | }
  18 |     | 
  19 |     | /// @title PositionNFT
  20 |     | /// @notice ERC-721 NFT representing isolated account containers in EqualLend pools
  21 |     | /// @dev Each NFT represents a position that can hold deposits, loans, and yield
  22 |     | contract PositionNFT is ERC721Enumerable, ReentrancyGuard {
  23 |     |     error PositionNFTHasOpenOffers(bytes32 positionKey);
  24 |     | 
  25 |     |     /// @notice Counter for generating unique token IDs
  26 |     |     uint256 public nextTokenId;
  27 |     | 
  28 |     |     /// @notice Mapping from token ID to pool ID
  29 |     |     mapping(uint256 => uint256) public tokenToPool;
  30 |     | 
  31 |     |     /// @notice Mapping from token ID to creation timestamp
  32 |     |     mapping(uint256 => uint40) public tokenCreationTime;
  33 |     | 
  34 |     |     /// @notice Emitted when a new Position NFT is minted
  35 |     |     /// @param tokenId The unique token ID
  36 |     |     /// @param owner The address that owns the NFT
  37 |     |     /// @param poolId The pool ID associated with this position
  38 |     |     event PositionMinted(uint256 indexed tokenId, address indexed owner, uint256 indexed poolId);
  39 |     | 
  40 |     |     /// @notice Constructor initializes the ERC721 token
  41 |     |     constructor() ERC721("EqualLend Position", "ELPOS") {
  42 |     |         nextTokenId = 1; // Start token IDs at 1
  43 |     |     }
  44 |     | 
  45 |     |     /// @notice Address authorized to mint Position NFTs (typically the PositionNFTFacet)
  46 |     |     address public minter;
  47 |     | 
  48 |     |     /// @notice Address of the Diamond contract for querying pool data
  49 |     |     address public diamond;
  50 |     | 
  51 |     |     /// @notice Emitted when the minter address is updated
  52 |     |     event MinterUpdated(address indexed oldMinter, address indexed newMinter);
  53 |     | 
  54 |     |     /// @notice Emitted when the diamond address is updated
  55 |     |     event DiamondUpdated(address indexed oldDiamond, address indexed newDiamond);
  56 |     | 
  57 |     |     /// @notice Set the authorized minter address
  58 |     |     /// @param _minter The new minter address
  59 |     |     function setMinter(address _minter) external {
  60 |     |         require(minter == address(0) || msg.sender == minter, "PositionNFT: unauthorized");
  61 |     |         address oldMinter = minter;
  62 |     |         minter = _minter;
  63 |     |         emit MinterUpdated(oldMinter, _minter);
  64 |     |     }
  65 |     | 
  66 |     |     /// @notice Set the Diamond contract address for pool data queries
  67 |     |     /// @param _diamond The Diamond contract address
  68 |     |     function setDiamond(address _diamond) external {
  69 |     |         require(diamond == address(0) || msg.sender == minter, "PositionNFT: unauthorized");
  70 |     |         address oldDiamond = diamond;
  71 |     |         diamond = _diamond;
  72 |     |         emit DiamondUpdated(oldDiamond, _diamond);
  73 |     |     }
  74 |     | 
  75 |     |     /// @notice Mint a new Position NFT for a specific pool
  76 |     |     /// @param to The address to mint the NFT to
  77 |     |     /// @param poolId The pool ID to associate with this position
  78 |     |     /// @return tokenId The newly minted token ID
  79 |     |     function mint(address to, uint256 poolId) 
  80 |     |         external 
  81 |     |         nonReentrant 
  82 |     |         returns (uint256 tokenId) 
  83 |     |     {
  84 |     |         require(msg.sender == minter, "PositionNFT: only minter");
  85 |     |         
  86 |     |         tokenId = nextTokenId++;
  87 |     |         
  88 |     |         _safeMint(to, tokenId);
  89 |     |         
  90 |     |         tokenToPool[tokenId] = poolId;
  91 |     |         tokenCreationTime[tokenId] = uint40(block.timestamp);
  92 |     |         
  93 |     |         emit PositionMinted(tokenId, to, poolId);
  94 |     |     }
  95 |     | 
  96 |     |     /// @notice Get the position key for a given token ID
  97 |     |     /// @dev Position key is used to index into PoolData mappings
  98 |     |     /// @param tokenId The token ID
  99 |     |     /// @return The derived position key
 100 |     |     function getPositionKey(uint256 tokenId) public view returns (bytes32) {
 101 |     |         if (!_exists(tokenId)) {
 102 |     |             revert InvalidTokenId(tokenId);
 103 |     |         }
 104 |     |         return LibPositionNFT.getPositionKey(address(this), tokenId);
 105 |     |     }
 106 |     | 
 107 |     |     /// @notice Get the pool ID associated with a token
 108 |     |     /// @param tokenId The token ID
 109 |     |     /// @return The pool ID
 110 |     |     function getPoolId(uint256 tokenId) external view returns (uint256) {
 111 |     |         if (!_exists(tokenId)) {
 112 |     |             revert InvalidTokenId(tokenId);
 113 |     |         }
 114 |     |         return tokenToPool[tokenId];
 115 |     |     }
 116 |     | 
 117 |     |     /// @notice Get the creation timestamp of a token
 118 |     |     /// @param tokenId The token ID
 119 |     |     /// @return The creation timestamp
 120 |     |     function getCreationTime(uint256 tokenId) external view returns (uint40) {
 121 |     |         if (!_exists(tokenId)) {
 122 |     |             revert InvalidTokenId(tokenId);
 123 |     |         }
 124 |     |         return tokenCreationTime[tokenId];
 125 |     |     }
 126 |     | 
 127 |     |     /// @notice Return the ERC-8004 agent registration file URI
 128 |     |     /// @param tokenId The token ID
 129 |     |     /// @return Registration file URI
 130 |     |     function tokenURI(uint256 tokenId) 
 131 |     |         public 
 132 |     |         view 
 133 |     |         override 
 134 |     |         returns (string memory) 
 135 |     |     {
 136 |     |         if (!_exists(tokenId)) {
 137 |     |             revert InvalidTokenId(tokenId);
 138 |     |         }
 139 |     |         if (diamond != address(0)) {
 140 |     |             return IAgentURIDiamond(diamond).getAgentURI(tokenId);
 141 |     |         }
 142 |     |         return super.tokenURI(tokenId);
 143 |     |     }
 144 |     | 
 145 |     |     /// @notice Check if a token exists
 146 |     |     /// @param tokenId The token ID to check
 147 |     |     /// @return True if the token exists
 148 |     |     function _exists(uint256 tokenId) internal view returns (bool) {
 149 |     |         return _ownerOf(tokenId) != address(0);
 150 |     |     }
 151 |     | 
 152 |     |     /// @notice Override supportsInterface to include ERC721Enumerable
 153 |     |     /// @param interfaceId The interface identifier
 154 |     |     /// @return True if the interface is supported
 155 |     |     function supportsInterface(bytes4 interfaceId) 
 156 |     |         public 
 157 |     |         view 
 158 |     |         virtual 
 159 |     |         override 
 160 |     |         returns (bool) 
 161 |     |     {
 162 |     |         return super.supportsInterface(interfaceId);
 163 |     |     }
 164 |     | 
 165 |     |     /// @notice Hook called during token transfers (mint, transfer, burn)
 166 |     |     /// @dev Position key remains unchanged during transfer - it's derived from (contract, tokenId)
 167 |     |     /// @dev All position data (principal, loans, yield) stays with the position key
 168 |     |     /// @dev New owner inherits all deposits and obligations associated with the NFT
 169 |     |     /// @param to The address receiving the token (address(0) for burning)
 170 |     |     /// @param tokenId The token being transferred
 171 |     |     /// @param auth The address authorized to perform the transfer
 172 |     |     /// @return from The previous owner of the token (address(0) for minting)
 173 |     |     function _update(
 174 |     |         address to,
 175 |     |         uint256 tokenId,
 176 |     |         address auth
 177 |     |     ) internal virtual override(ERC721Enumerable) returns (address) {
 178 |     |         address from = super._update(to, tokenId, auth);
 179 |     |         
 180 |     |         // Position key derivation: address(uint160(uint256(keccak256(abi.encodePacked(nftContract, tokenId)))))
 181 |     |         // This key is deterministic and depends only on the contract address and token ID
 182 |     |         // Therefore, it remains unchanged when ownership transfers from 'from' to 'to'
 183 |     |         
 184 |     |         // All position data in PoolData mappings uses this position key:
 185 |     |         // - userPrincipal[positionKey]: deposit amount
 186 |     |         // - userFeeIndex[positionKey]: fee-index checkpoint
 187 |     |         // - userMaintenanceIndex[positionKey]: maintenance-index checkpoint
 188 |     |         // - userAccruedYield[positionKey]: accrued yield
 189 |     |         // - externalCollateral[positionKey]: external collateral
 190 |     |         // - rollingLoans[positionKey]: rolling credit loan
 191 |     |         // - fixedTermLoans[loanId]: fixed-term loans (where borrower = positionKey)
 192 |     |         // - userFixedLoanIds[positionKey]: array of loan IDs
 193 |     |         
 194 |     |         // Since the position key doesn't change, all this data automatically transfers
 195 |     |         // to the new owner. The new owner can operate on the NFT and access all
 196 |     |         // deposits, loans, and yield associated with it.
 197 |     |         
 198 |     |         // Block transfers while outstanding direct offers exist (checked via the diamond, if set).
 199 |     |         if (from != address(0) && to != address(0) && from != to && diamond != address(0)) {
 200 |     |             bytes32 positionKey = LibPositionNFT.getPositionKey(address(this), tokenId);
 201 |     |             if (IDirectOfferCanceller(diamond).hasOpenOffers(positionKey)) {
 202 |     |                 revert PositionNFTHasOpenOffers(positionKey);
 203 |     |             }
 204 |     |         }
 205 |     |         
 206 |     |         return from;
 207 |     |     }
 208 |     | 
 209 |     | }
 210 |     |

 144 |     |     // ========== INVARIANTS ==========
 145 |     | 
 146 |     |     /// @notice Solvency: Contract balance must cover all vault balances + fee pots.
 147 |     |     function echidna_solvency() public view returns (bool) {
 148 |     |         EqualIndexStorage storage store = s();
 149 |     |         
 150 |     |         // Check Token A
 151 |     |         uint256 vaultA = store.vaultBalances[INDEX_ID][address(tokenA)];
 152 |     |         uint256 potA = store.feePots[INDEX_ID][address(tokenA)];
 153 |     |         uint256 actualA = tokenA.balanceOf(address(this));
 154 |     |         
 155 |     |         // We might have extra tokens from "minting to self" that weren't fully used due to rounding/buffer
 156 |     |         // So actual >= liability
 157 |     |         if (actualA < vaultA + potA) return false;
 158 |     |         
 159 |     |         // Check Token B
 160 |     |         uint256 vaultB = store.vaultBalances[INDEX_ID][address(tokenB)];
 161 |     |         uint256 potB = store.feePots[INDEX_ID][address(tokenB)];
 162 |     |         uint256 actualB = tokenB.balanceOf(address(this));
 163 |     |         
 164 |     |         if (actualB < vaultB + potB) return false;
 165 |     |         
 166 |     |         return true;
 167 |     |     }
 168 |     |     
 169 |     |     /// @notice Backing: Total Supply * Bundle Amount <= Vault Balance * Scale
 170 |     |     /// (Strict backing check, assuming no external donations)
 171 |     |     function echidna_backing_integrity() public view returns (bool) {
 172 |     |         EqualIndexStorage storage store = s();
 173 |     |         uint256 supply = indexToken.totalSupply();
 174 |     |         
 175 |     |         uint256 vaultA = store.vaultBalances[INDEX_ID][address(tokenA)];
 176 |     |         uint256 reqA = (supply * 1 ether) / 1e18;
 177 |     |         
 178 |     |         if (reqA > vaultA) return false;
 179 |     |         
 180 |     |         uint256 vaultB = store.vaultBalances[INDEX_ID][address(tokenB)];
 181 |     |         uint256 reqB = (supply * 2 ether) / 1e18;
 182 |     |         if (reqB > vaultB) return false;
 183 |     |         
 184 |     |         return true;
 185 |     |     }
 186 |     |     
 187 |     |     /// @notice Fee Pot Monotonicity (loosely): Fee pots should not decrease unless burned?
 188 |     |     /// Actually, burn redemptions CLAIM from the fee pot. So they can decrease.
 189 |     |     /// But they shouldn't go negative (Solidity handles underflow).
 190 |     |     
 191 |     |     /// @notice Fee Pot Consistency:
 192 |     |     /// If supply > 0, fee pot claims should be proportional.
 193 |     |     
 194 |     | }
 195 |     |
